  1. [2610.10575] 有界数量曲率的Ricci流在7维以下的L2曲率界
    $L^2$ Curvature Bounds on Ricci flows with bounded scalar curvature up to dimension 7
    👥 Xianbin Huang, Wancheng Zhang
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 研究有界数量曲率Ricci流上的曲率估计问题。
    • 通过发展Lp颈部分解定理和颈部超凸性估计，克服了逐点控制的缺失。
    • 建立了直到7维的一致L2黎曼曲率界和L4 Ricci曲率界。
    • 推广了Jiang-Naber的L2曲率估计，并应用于有界数量曲率Ricci流。
    💡 在数量曲率一致有界的Ricci流上，建立了直到7维的一致L2黎曼曲率界和L4 Ricci曲率界。
    📖 中文摘要

    本文研究具有一致有界数量曲率的Ricci流。在此类Ricci流上，我们建立了直到7维的一致L2黎曼曲率界和L4 Ricci曲率界。这些估计改进了Bamler建立的L2-ε曲率界和Bamler与Zhang在4维建立的L2曲率界。更一般地，我们在满足积分Lp Ricci曲率界（p>n/2）、一致体积下界和强ε-正则性假设的n维流形上建立了统一L2曲率界。该结果推广了Jiang和Naber在逐点双边Ricci曲率界下建立的著名L2曲率估计。为克服逐点控制的缺失，我们发展了颈部分解定理的Lp适应版本，并建立了颈部区域曲率的超凸性估计。此外，我们证明了在具有有界数量曲率的Ricci流上，我们的结构假设是动态自然的。作为应用，结合Bamler建立的Ricci流先验L4-ε曲率半径界，我们得到了维数n≤7的有界数量曲率Ricci流上的一致L2曲率界。

    🌐 English Abstract

    In this paper, we study Ricci flows with uniformly bounded scalar curvature. On such Ricci flows we establish uniform $L^2$ Riemannian curvature bounds and $L^4$ Ricci curvature bounds up to dimension 7. These estimates improves $L^{2-\varepsilon}$ curvature bounds established by Bamler and $L^2$ curvature bounds in dimension 4 established by Bamler and Zhang. More generally, we establish uniform $L^2$ curvature bounds on $n$-manifolds satisfying an integral $L^p$ Ricci curvature bound for $p> n/2$, a uniform volume lower bound, and a strong $\varepsilon$-regularity assumption. This result extends the celebrated $L^2$ curvature estimates of Jiang and Naber, which were established under pointwise two-sided Ricci curvature bounds. To overcome the lack of pointwise control, we develop an $L^p$ adaptation of the neck decomposition theorem and establish a superconvexity estimate for the curvature on neck regions. Furthermore, we demonstrate that our structural assumptions are dynamically natural on Ricci flows with bounded scalar curvature. As an application, by combining our estimate with the a priori $L^{4-\varepsilon}$ curvature radius bound of Ricci flow established by Bamler, we obtain uniform $L^2$ curvature bounds on Ricci flows with bounded scalar curvature of dimension $n \le 7$.

    👉 https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10575
  2. [2610.10634] 拓扑球面λ-自膨胀子的例子
    Examples of topological sphere $\lambda$-self-expander
    👥 Mengdan Qi
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 研究加权等周问题中的λ-自膨胀子。
    • 对充分大的λ，构造了R^{n+1}中浸入非嵌入的球面λ-自膨胀子，且平均曲率为正。
    • 给出了n=2时的显式解。
    💡 对充分大的λ，构造了R^{n+1}中具有正平均曲率的浸入非嵌入球面λ-自膨胀子，并给出n=2的显式解。
    📖 中文摘要

    λ-自膨胀子F: M^n→R^{n+1}是权重e^{|x|^2/4}的等周问题的解。本文中，对于充分大的λ，我们在R^{n+1}中构造了一个具有正平均曲率H>0的浸入非嵌入球面λ-自膨胀子。此外，我们得到了n=2时的显式解。

    🌐 English Abstract

    A $\lambda$-self-expander $F: M^n\to \mathbb{R}^{n+1}$ is the solution of the isoperimetric problem of weight $e^{\frac{|x|^2}{4}}$. In this paper, for sufficiently large $\lambda$, we construct an immersed non-embedded spherical $\lambda$-self-expander in $\mathbb{R}^{n+1}$ with the mean curvature $H>0$. Moreover, we obtain the explicit solution for $n=2$.

    👉 https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10634
  3. [2610.10754] 极小超曲面的柱状奇点
    On Cylindrical singularities of minimal hypersurfaces
    👥 Aria Halavati, Luca Spolaor
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 研究极小超曲面奇点的结构。
    • 在R^9中构造了一个具有孤立奇点序列收敛到原点的极小超曲面。
    • 每个孤立奇点处切锥为C_{4,3}，原点处切锥为C_{3,3}×R。
    • 关键步骤是构造具有指定切锥的整体稳定超曲面。
    💡 在R^9中构造了一个极小超曲面，其奇点集包含原点及收敛到原点的孤立奇点序列，并具有特定的切锥。
    📖 中文摘要

    在R^9中原点的邻域内，我们构造了一个关于光滑共形欧几里得度量的极小超曲面，其奇点集由原点和收敛到原点的孤立奇点序列组成。在每个孤立奇点处，超曲面具有唯一的切锥C_{4,3}，即S^4(√(4/7))×S^3(√(3/7))上的锥；而在原点处，其唯一切锥是C_{3,3}×R。该度量在每个孤立奇点附近是欧几里得的，并且在原点处与欧几里得度量无限阶一致。一个具有独立意义的关键步骤是构造欧几里得R^9中一个整体稳定超曲面，它恰有一个奇点位于原点，其唯一切锥为C_{4,3}，且无穷远处的唯一切锥为C_{3,3}×R。

    🌐 English Abstract

    In a neighborhood of the origin in $\mathbb{R}^9$, we construct a minimal hypersurface with respect to a smooth conformally Euclidean metric, whose singular set consists of the origin and a sequence of isolated singular points converging to it. At each of the isolated singular points the hypersurface has the unique tangent cone $C_{4,3}$, the cone over $S^4(\sqrt{4/7})\times S^3(\sqrt{3/7})$, whereas at the origin its unique tangent cone is the cone $C_{3,3}\times\mathbb{R}$. The metric is Euclidean near each isolated singular point and agrees with the Euclidean metric to infinite order at the origin. A key step, of independent interest, is the construction of an entire stationary hypersurface in Euclidean $\mathbb{R}^9$ with exactly one singular point at the origin, at which its unique tangent cone is $C_{4,3}$, and whose unique tangent cone at infinity is $C_{3,3}\times\mathbb{R}$.

    👉 https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10754
  4. [2610.10790] 具有浸入特殊勒让德链环的特殊拉格朗日切锥的唯一性
    Uniqueness of tangent cones with immersed special Legendrian links
    👥 Yang Li
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 研究特殊拉格朗日切锥的唯一性问题。
    • 在Jacobi场可积和链环相交模式条件下，证明了切锥的唯一性。
    • 证明基于离散Lojasiewicz型不等式和粘合障碍机制。
    💡 在光滑Jacobi场可积和链环相交模式条件下，证明了C^3中具有浸入特殊勒让德链环的特殊拉格朗日切锥的唯一性。
    📖 中文摘要

    在光滑Jacobi场可积的假设和链环相交模式条件下，我们证明了C^3中特殊拉格朗日切锥的唯一性，这些切锥的链环是具有横截双点的紧浸入特殊勒让德曲面。证明基于一个离散Lojasiewicz型不等式，该不等式来自粘合障碍机制。

    🌐 English Abstract

    Under the assumption of integrability of the smooth Jacobi fields and a condition on the intersection pattern of the link, we prove uniqueness of special Lagrangian tangent cones in $\mathbb{C}^3$ whose links are compact immersed special Legendrian surfaces with transverse double points. The proof is based on a discrete Lojasiewicz type inequality, which comes from a gluing obstruction mechanism.

    👉 https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10790
  5. [2610.10796] 具有凸单位切迭代的非圆卵形线
    A noncircular oval with convex unit-tangent iterates
    👥 Dean Matthew Menezes
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 研究平面曲线单位切迭代的凸性问题。
    • 构造了一条非圆曲线，其所有迭代都是光滑嵌入闭曲线且曲率处处为正。
    • 通过构造非紧凸曲线并利用一致逆估计，将近似关系转化为精确序列。
    • 回答了Tabachnikov的问题。
    💡 构造了一条非圆曲线，使得其所有单位切迭代都是光滑嵌入闭曲线且曲率处处为正，回答了Tabachnikov的问题。
    📖 中文摘要

    对于有向正则平面曲线γ，定义Tγ=γ+τ，其中τ是其单位切向量。我们构造了一条非圆曲线Γ_0，使得每个迭代T^nΓ_0（n≥0）都是光滑嵌入闭曲线且曲率处处为正。这回答了Tabachnikov的一个问题。构造从一条非紧凸曲线C开始，该曲线在其两端渐近于平行线，并满足TC=C+(V,0)（V>0）。从C我们构造长闭凸曲线。在匹配周长后，我们将每条曲线与一条凸曲线比较，该凸曲线的单位切像序列中的下一条曲线。在适当的弧长坐标中，它们的曲率函数在L1中相差指数小量。一致逆估计将这些近似关系转化为精确序列Γ_{n+1}=TΓ_n。每个Γ_n位于宽度独立于n的有界带中，而迭代圆的半径趋于无穷。

    🌐 English Abstract

    For an oriented regular plane curve $\gamma$, define ${\cal T}\gamma=\gamma+\tau$, where $\tau$ is its unit tangent. We construct a noncircular curve $\Gamma_0$ such that every iterate ${\cal T}^n\Gamma_0$, $n\geq0$, is a smooth embedded closed curve with everywhere positive curvature. This answers a question of Tabachnikov. The construction starts with a noncompact convex curve $C$, asymptotic at its two ends to parallel lines, satisfying ${\cal T} C=C+(V,0)$ for some $V>0$. From $C$ we construct long closed convex curves. After matching perimeters, we compare each curve with a convex curve whose unit-tangent image is the next curve in the sequence. In suitable arclength coordinates, their curvature functions differ by an exponentially small amount in $L^1$. Uniform inverse estimates turn these approximate relations into an exact sequence $\Gamma_{n+1}={\cal T}\Gamma_n$. Each $\Gamma_n$ lies in a strip of width bounded independently of $n$, whereas the radii of iterated circles tend to infinity.

    👉 https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10796
  6. [2610.10887] 四维中两类曲率算子
    Two Kinds of Curvature Operators in Dimension Four
    👥 Xiaodong Cao, Xiaolong Li
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 研究四维中两类曲率算子之间的关系。
    • 证明了第一类和第二类曲率算子的不变恒等式，并联系了双正交截面曲率。
    • 分类了满足K^⊥_max≤S/6的闭定向四维流形，包括数量曲率为零的情况。
    💡 证明了四维中第一类和第二类曲率算子的不变恒等式，并分类了满足K^⊥_max≤S/6的闭定向四维流形。
    📖 中文摘要

    我们证明了四维中第一类和第二类曲率算子的一个不变恒等式。在自然同构S^2_0≅Λ^+⊗Λ^-下，无迹Ricci张量通过一个交换子项贡献。作为推论，我们将单位自对偶和反自对偶二形式乘积上的第二类曲率算子与双正交截面曲率联系起来。然后我们分类了满足K^⊥_max≤S/6的闭定向四维流形，其中K^⊥_max表示最大双正交截面曲率，S是数量曲率。分类包括数量曲率为零的情况。

    🌐 English Abstract

    We prove an invariant identity relating the curvature operators of the first and second kind in dimension four. Under the natural identification $S^2_0\cong\Lambda^+\otimes\Lambda^-$, the trace-free Ricci tensor contributes through a single commutator term. As a consequence, we relate the curvature operator of the second kind on products of unit self-dual and anti-self-dual two-forms to biorthogonal sectional curvature. We then classify closed oriented four-manifolds satisfying $K^\perp_{\max}\leq S/6$, where $K^\perp_{\max}$ denotes the maximum biorthogonal sectional curvature and $S$ is the scalar curvature. The classification includes the cases in which the scalar curvature vanishes.

    👉 https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10887
  7. [2610.10902] 广义旗流形余切丛的典范辛形式描述
    A description of the canonical symplectic form of the cotangent bundle of a generalized flag manifold
    👥 Juan Fervenza, Lino Grama, Luiz A. B. San Martin
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 研究广义旗流形余切丛的辛几何。
    • 用主丛的连接和曲率形式显式描述了Liouville辛形式。
    • 建立了李理论性质与辛结构之间的几何联系。
    💡 用主丛的连接和曲率形式显式描述了广义旗流形余切丛上的Liouville辛形式，建立了李理论性质与辛结构的几何联系。
    📖 中文摘要

    我们研究与半单李群相关的齐性空间的辛几何，特别关注广义旗流形的余切丛。我们的方法用自然来自李群结构的主丛的连接和曲率形式显式描述了这些空间上的Liouville辛形式。这提供了广义旗流形的李理论性质与其余切丛所携带的辛结构之间的几何联系。

    🌐 English Abstract

    We investigate the symplectic geometry of homogeneous spaces associated with semisimple Lie groups, with particular emphasis on cotangent bundles of generalized flag manifolds. Our approach gives an explicit description of the Liouville symplectic form on these spaces in terms of the connections and curvature forms of principal bundles naturally arising from the Lie group structure. This provides a geometric link between the Lie-theoretic properties of generalized flag manifolds and the symplectic structures carried by their cotangent bundles.

    👉 https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10902
  8. [2610.10908] 无处局部度量极小的鞍面
    Nowhere Locally Metric-Minimising Saddle Surfaces
    👥 Stefan Christian Kohlmeier
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 研究鞍面与局部度量极小性的关系。
    • 证明了R^4中严格鞍嵌入可由无处局部度量极小的鞍嵌入逼近。
    • 确认了Petrunin和Stadler的预期，并构造了非平凡无穷小等距变形。
    • 提供了显式扭曲二次曲面的例子。
    💡 证明了R^4中每个光滑严格鞍嵌入闭圆盘都可由无处局部度量极小的鞍嵌入逼近，从而确认了Petrunin和Stadler的预期。
    📖 中文摘要

    我们研究欧几里得空间中光滑曲面的鞍性质与局部度量极小性之间的关系。Anton Petrunin和Stephan Stadler证明了R^3中每个光滑严格鞍面是局部度量极小的，并预期这一现象在R^4中不成立。我们确认了这一预期，并证明了一个更强的稠密性结果：R^4中闭圆盘的每个光滑严格鞍嵌入都可在C^∞拓扑下由无处局部度量极小的光滑严格鞍嵌入任意逼近。特别地，对于每个d≥4，R^d中存在无处局部度量极小的鞍嵌入。主要工具是构造R^4中鞍浸入的非平凡无穷小等距变形，其支集在任意小的圆盘中。我们还通过推导度量极小性的必要条件，提供了一个显式的扭曲二次曲面，它在原点附近是鞍面但在该处不是局部度量极小的。

    🌐 English Abstract

    We study the relation between the saddle property and local metric minimality for smooth surfaces in Euclidean spaces. Anton Petrunin and Stephan Stadler proved that every smooth strictly saddle surface in $\mathbb{R}^3$ is locally metric-minimising and expressed the expectation that this phenomenon fails in $\mathbb{R}^4$. We confirm this expectation and prove a stronger density result: Every smooth strictly saddle embedding of a closed disc into $\mathbb{R}^4$ can be approximated arbitrarily closely in the $C^\infty$-topology by smooth strictly saddle embeddings that are nowhere locally metric-minimising. In particular, nowhere locally metric-minimising saddle embeddings exist in $\mathbb{R}^d$ for every $d \geq 4$. The main ingredient is the construction of non-trivial infinitesimal isometric deformations of saddle immersions in $\mathbb{R}^4$ supported in arbitrarily small discs. We also provide an explicit twisted quadratic surface that is saddle in a neighbourhood of the origin but fails to be locally metric-minimising there, by deriving a necessary condition for metric minimality.

    👉 https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10908
  9. [2610.10937] 三维可解李群上的适应复极化
    Adapted complex polarizations on solvable Lie groups of dimension 3
    👥 Balázs Forman
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 研究可解李群上适应复极化的存在性。
    • 利用测地流的iR-完备性准则和Euler-Arnold向量场，构造了若干左不变ac-极化的例子。
    • 主要结果是展示了非局部对称但P-完备的连接。
    💡 研究了可解李群上左不变Koszul连接的适应复极化存在性，给出了若干非局部对称但P-完备连接的例子。
    📖 中文摘要

    本文研究实李群上左不变Koszul连接相关的适应复极化。适应复极化作为流形上复结构的自然推广出现。我们研究在某些具有左不变连接的李群的切丛上此类极化的存在性。我们使用一个实用准则，通过连接的测地流的iR-完备性来判断全局适应复极化的存在性。在李群情形，Euler-Arnold向量场决定了测地流的行为。通过结合关于某些李群的Euler-Arnold向量场的已知结果和iR-完备性准则，我们提供了若干定义在李群切丛上的左不变ac-极化的例子。除了伪黎曼度量连接，我们还讨论了几种情况下的双不变连接。我们的重点是可解李群，因为非可解李群已被更好地理解。本文的主要结果是展示了若干非局部对称但P-完备的连接。

    🌐 English Abstract

    In this paper we investigate adapted complex polarizations associated with left-invariant Koszul connections on real Lie groups. Adapted complex polarizations arise as a natural generalization of a complex structure on a manifold. We investigate the existence of such polarizations on the tangent bundle of certain Lie groups with left-invariant connections. We use a practical criterion for the existence of global adapted complex polarizations in terms of the $i\mathbb R$-completeness of the geodesic flow of the connection. In the Lie case, the Euler-Arnold vector field determines the behaviour of the geodesic flow. By combining what we know about the Euler-Arnold vector field of certain Lie groups and the $i\mathbb R$-completeness criterion, we provide several examples of left-invariant ac-polarizations defined on the tangent bundle of Lie groups. Along with pseudo-Riemannian metric connections, we discuss biinvariant connections in several cases. Our focus is on solvable Lie groups, because non-solvable Lie groups are better understood. The main result of this paper is that we demonstrate several not locally symmetric but $\mathcal P$-complete connections.

    👉 https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10937
  10. [2610.11040] 具有拼挤曲率的四维收缩子
    Four-dimensional shrinkers with pinched curvatures
    👥 Xiaodong Cao, Jia-Yong Wu
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 研究四维收缩子的分类和曲率拼挤。
    • 在第二Ricci曲率拼挤下分类了完备四维收缩子。
    • 在半Weyl曲率积分拼挤和双正交曲率假设下分类了闭四维收缩子。
    • 建立了闭非平凡四维收缩子的Hitchin-Thorpe型不等式。
    💡 在第二Ricci曲率拼挤条件下分类了完备四维收缩子，并在半Weyl曲率积分拼挤下分类了闭四维收缩子，建立了Hitchin-Thorpe型不等式。
    📖 中文摘要

    本文中，我们首先在第二Ricci曲率的拼挤条件下分类了完备四维收缩子。然后，我们在双正交曲率的若干拼挤假设下，分类了具有半Weyl曲率（加权）积分参数拼挤的闭四维收缩子。最后，我们在半Weyl曲率和双正交曲率的某些假设下，为闭非平凡四维收缩子建立了一个Hitchin-Thorpe型不等式。

    🌐 English Abstract

    In this paper, we first classify complete four-dimensional shrinkers under a pinching condition on the $2$nd-Ricci curvature. We then classify closed four-dimensional shrinkers with (weighted) integral parameter-pinching of the half Weyl curvature under several pinching assumptions on the biorthogonal curvature. Finally, we establish a Hitchin-Thorpe type inequality for closed non-trivial four-dimensional shrinkers under some assumptions on the half Weyl curvature and the biorthogonal curvature.

    👉 https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11040
  11. [2610.11077] Kummer K3曲面上显式Ricci平坦度量
    Explicit Ricci-flat Metrics on Kummer K3 Surfaces
    👥 Jixiang Fu, Yunyang Xiao, Shing-Tung Yau
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 研究Kummer K3曲面上Ricci平坦Kähler度量的显式构造。
    • 通过Green算子和Neumann级数给出了收敛的显式表示。
    • 证明了级数绝对收敛，并定义了Calabi-Yau势。
    • 记录了在坍缩椭圆K3曲面上递归的解析条件。
    💡 在固定Kummer K3曲面上的一族类中，给出了Calabi-Yau定理提供的Ricci平坦Kähler度量的收敛显式表示。
    📖 中文摘要

    我们给出了在固定Kummer K3曲面上的一族类中，Calabi-Yau定理提供的Ricci平坦Kähler度量的收敛显式表示。一个显式的径向背景具有归一化的Monge-Ampère残差O(a^4)。其标量Green算子G_a由周期Ewald核、分离径向核、有限维Schur补和收敛Neumann级数表示。系数由U_{a,1}=-G_a f_a和U_{a,n}=G_a∑_{j=1}^{n-1}Q_a(U_{a,j},U_{a,n-j})定义，其中Q_a是极化二次Monge-Ampère项。一个a^{-1}阶的Green估计给出了a^3阶的第一修正和a^2阶的收敛参数。一个Catalan优级数证明了对于每个充分小的固定a，在整个光滑曲面上绝对收敛。该和定义了一个正形式，解体积方程；比较将该和与光滑归一化Calabi-Yau势等同。我们还记录了在坍缩椭圆K3曲面上相同递归的充分解析条件。

    🌐 English Abstract

    We give a convergent explicit representation of the Ricci-flat K\"ahler metrics supplied by the Calabi--Yau theorem in a one-parameter family of classes on a fixed Kummer K3 surface. An explicit radial background has normalized Monge--Amp\`ere residual \(O(a^4)\). Its scalar Green operator \(G_a\) is represented by periodic Ewald kernels, separated radial kernels, finite-dimensional Schur complements, and convergent Neumann series. The coefficients are defined by \(U_{a,1}=-G_af_a\) and \(U_{a,n}=G_a\sum_{j=1}^{n-1}Q_a(U_{a,j},U_{a,n-j})\), where \(Q_a\) is the polarized quadratic Monge--Amp\`ere term. A Green estimate of order \(a^{-1}\) gives a first correction of order \(a^3\) and a convergence parameter of order \(a^2\). A Catalan majorant proves absolute convergence on the entire smooth surface for every sufficiently small fixed \(a\). The sum defines a positive form solving the volume equation; comparison identifies the sum with the smooth normalized Calabi--Yau potential. We also record sufficient analytic conditions for the same recursion on collapsing elliptic K3 surfaces.

    👉 https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11077
  12. [2610.11209] 水平超曲面具有平行第二基本形式的Ricci孤子
    On Ricci solitons whose level hypersurfaces have parallel second fundamental form
    👥 Matheus Andrade Ribeiro de Moura Horácio
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 研究水平超曲面具有平行第二基本形式的Ricci孤子。
    • 证明了此类孤子局部为多重扭曲积结构，势函数仅依赖于基。
    • 给出了刚性结果，包括Cao猜想在特定条件下的成立。
    • 推广到仅一个正则水平超曲面具有该性质的情形。
    💡 研究了势函数水平超曲面具有平行第二基本形式的梯度Ricci孤子，证明了其局部为多重扭曲积结构，并给出了刚性结果。
    📖 中文摘要

    我们研究势函数的水平超曲面具有平行第二基本形式的梯度Ricci孤子。我们证明这样的孤子局部是一维基和Einstein纤维的多重扭曲积，势函数仅依赖于基。因此，局部地，这一条件刻画了许多经典Ricci孤子构造中出现的多重扭曲结构。我们进一步证明，对于任何梯度Ricci孤子，只要一个正则水平超曲面具有平行第二基本形式和平行内蕴Ricci张量，同样的局部结构也成立。作为应用，我们证明一个具有常数量曲率的完备非稳态梯度Ricci孤子，只要一个正则水平超曲面具有此性质，就是刚性的。特别地，Cao的猜想对于具有此性质的梯度收缩Ricci孤子成立。我们还证明了一个完备梯度收缩Ricci孤子，其正则水平超曲面具有平行第二基本形式且至多一个非零主曲率，则无需数量曲率假设就是刚性的。

    🌐 English Abstract

    We study gradient Ricci solitons whose potential functions have level hypersurfaces with parallel second fundamental form. We show that such a soliton is locally a multiply warped product of a one-dimensional base and Einstein fibers, with potential function depending only on the base. Thus, locally, this condition characterizes the multiply warped structure that occurs in many classical constructions of Ricci solitons. We further prove that the same local structure follows for any gradient Ricci soliton if just one regular level hypersurface has parallel second fundamental form and parallel intrinsic Ricci tensor. As an application, we prove that a complete nonsteady gradient Ricci soliton with constant scalar curvature is rigid as soon as one regular level hypersurface has this property. In particular, the conjecture of Cao holds for gradient shrinking Ricci solitons with this property. We also prove that a complete gradient shrinking Ricci soliton whose regular level hypersurfaces have parallel second fundamental form and at most one nonzero principal curvature is rigid, without any assumption on the scalar curvature.

    👉 https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11209

    📖 中文摘要

We also show that this inequality is closely related to a family of monotonicity formulae, extending previous results of Park to the setting of positive curvature. We further present several geometric applications, such as a sharp comparison inequality for the sum of two distinct Green's distance functions.

    📖 中文摘要

    本文研究后向平坦非线性离散时间系统的基于平坦性的跟踪控制器设计。首先，我们证明通过将隐式欧拉离散化应用于结构平坦三角表示，可以从微分平坦连续时间系统构造性地获得后向平坦离散时间系统。随后，考虑了后向平坦系统精确线性化的两种方法。除了基于预延拓的动态反馈外，我们证明可以使用涉及平坦输出低阶后向移位的线性化反馈，而无需显式实现相应的动态扩展。这允许降低所得跟踪误差动态的阶数，同时仅需要过去系统轨迹的存储值。基于所得的线性输入输出表示，推导了基于平坦性的跟踪控制律。所提出的离散化和控制器设计以二维龙门起重机为例进行了说明。

    This paper addresses the design of flatness-based tracking controllers for backward-flat nonlinear discrete-time systems. First, we show that backward-flat discrete-time systems can be constructively obtained from differentially flat continuous-time systems by applying an implicit Euler discretization to a structurally flat triangular representation. Subsequently, two approaches for the exact linearization of backward-flat systems are considered. Besides a dynamic feedback based on prelongations, we show that a linearizing feedback involving lower-order backward-shifts of the flat output can be employed without explicitly implementing the corresponding dynamic extension. This allows the order of the resulting tracking error dynamics to be reduced while requiring only stored values of past system trajectories. Based on the resulting linear input-output representation, flatness-based tracking control laws are derived. The proposed discretization and controller design are illustrated for a 2D gantry crane.

We prove that $\sum\limits_{\alpha=1}^{\min\{n,m\}}\lambda_\alpha+\lambda_2\leq n$ forces $M$ to be a totally geodesic subsphere, a generalized Clifford torus, or a Veronese manifold. We first show that equality everywhere in the pinching condition forces the second fundamental form to be parallel. Next, by computing the eigenvalues of the fundamental matrices of irreducible symmetric $R$-spaces, we obtain a complete classification of the equality cases among minimal submanifolds with parallel second fundamental form.

    👥 Amadou Sy, Ameth Ndiaye, Ghodratallah Fasihi-Ramandi
    🤖 AI 总结

    • 在单位切丛上，Killing场李代数为sl(2,R)⊕R，不存在Ricci孤子，但具有η-Einstein性质。

    💡 研究了双曲带及其单位切丛上的Killing向量场、Ricci孤子和双曲Ricci孤子，证明了单位切丛上不存在Ricci孤子，并给出了η-Ricci孤子的结果。
    📖 中文摘要

    我们研究双曲带S_a={(x,y)∈R^2:0<y<a}及其单位切丛T^1S_a上的Killing向量场、Ricci孤子和双曲Ricci孤子，其中S_a配备度量g_{S_a}=π^2/(a^2 sin^2(πy/a))(dx^2+dy^2)，T^1S_a配备Sasaki度量g^S。在带上，我们证明Gauss曲率恒为-1，Killing场的李代数同构于sl(2,R)，每个Ricci孤子都是平凡的（Killing势，λ=-1），并且一个共形的2-Killing场，或是具有λ≠0的双曲Ricci孤子的势，是Killing场。在单位切丛上，我们证明(T^1S_a,g^S)的Killing场李代数是四维的，同构于sl(2,R)⊕R，g^S不是Einstein的但具有常系数的η-Einstein性质，Ric^S=-3/2 g^S+2 η⊗η，且(T^1S_a,g^S)不存在Ricci孤子。对于双曲Ricci孤子，我们证明了Killing、左不变和具有共形投影的θ-不变势的不存在性，并将一般θ-不变情形归结为带上的方程组；任意势的分类仍未解决。最后，我们证明g^S的η-Ricci孤子具有Killing势且λ=3/2，μ=-2，并且在相同势类中得到的η-双曲Ricci孤子是平凡的，具有Killing势，μ=-3/2和ν=2。

    🌐 English Abstract

    We study Killing vector fields, Ricci solitons and hyperbolic Ricci solitons on the hyperbolic strip $S_a=\{(x,y)\in\mathbb{R}^2:0<y<a\}$ endowed with the metric $g_{S_{a}}=\frac{\pi^2}{a^2\sin^2(\pi y/a)}(dx^2+dy^2)$, and on its unit tangent bundle $T^1S_a$ endowed with the Sasaki metric $g^S$. On the strip, we show that the Gauss curvature is constant equal to $-1$, that the Lie algebra of Killing fields is isomorphic to $\mathfrak{sl}(2,\mathbb{R})$, that every Ricci soliton is trivial (Killing potential, $\lambda=-1$), and that a $2$-Killing field which is conformal, or which is the potential of a hyperbolic Ricci soliton with $\lambda\neq0$, is a Killing field. On the unit tangent bundle, we prove that the Lie algebra of Killing fields of $(T^1S_a,g^S)$ is four-dimensional and isomorphic to $\mathfrak{sl}(2,\mathbb{R})\oplus\mathbb{R}$, that $g^S$ is not Einstein but is $\eta$-Einstein with constant coefficients, $\operatorname{Ric}^S=-\tfrac32\,g^S+2\,\eta\otimes\eta$, and that $(T^1S_a,g^S)$ admits no Ricci soliton. For hyperbolic Ricci solitons we prove non-existence for Killing, left-invariant, and $\theta$-invariant potentials with conformal projection, and we reduce the general $\theta$-invariant case to a system of equations on the strip; the classification for an arbitrary potential is left open. Finally, we show that the $\eta$-Ricci solitons of $g^S$ have a Killing potential and $\lambda=\tfrac32$, $\mu=-2$, and that the $\eta$-hyperbolic Ricci solitons obtained in the same classes of potentials are the trivial ones, with a Killing potential, $\mu=-\tfrac32$ and $\nu=2$.

We demonstrate that a compact Riemannian manifold with nonnegative sectional curvature and nonnegative complete divergence of the Weyl curvature tensor, which admits a nontrivial solution to the critical point equation, must be Einstein and is isometric to a standard sphere.     We prove the Hawking and Penrose singularity theorems for Lorentz--Finsler structures of mixed horizontal/vertical regularity $C^{(1,2)}$ in the $C^1$ weighted case and $C^{(1,3)}$ in the unweighted case. At this regularity, geodesics need not be uniquely determined by their initial conditions, and the (weighted) Ricci curvature must be interpreted distributionally. Our approach relies on a two-stage approximation procedure: first, a homogeneity-preserving convolution, which is of independent interest in the broader semi-Riemann--Finsler setting, and second, a causality-adapted approximation \`a la Chru\'sciel--Grant.     👥 Matheus Horácio, Wenqi Li, Jianyu Ou, Guoqiang Wu, Detang Zhou
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 研究常数量曲率对Kähler-Ricci孤子刚性的影响。利用加权截断和Codazzi对称性证明刚性。得到孤子全纯等距分裂为Kähler-Einstein部分和欧几里得空间。给出了Riemannian刚性判据，无需数量曲率假设。
    💡 证明了常数量曲率迫使非稳态梯度Kähler-Ricci孤子刚性，并给出了Riemannian刚性判据。
    📖 中文摘要

    设(M^{2m},g,f,J)是满足Ric+∇^2 f=λ g（λ≠0）的完备非稳态梯度Kähler-Ricci孤子。我们证明常数量曲率迫使孤子刚性。更精确地，万有覆盖全纯且等距地分裂为N^{2k}×C^{m-k}，其中N^{2k}是Kähler-Einstein的且Ric_{g_N}=λ g_N。在收缩情形商是平凡的；在归一化λ=1/2时，有R≡k。Kähler结果基于一个Riemannian刚性判据。在完备非稳态梯度Ricci孤子上，如果L_{∇f}Ric处处非负或非正，则它消失且孤子刚性；不需要关于数量曲率的假设。沿着由孤子生成的Ricci流，这意味着在时间上单调的Ricci张量在时间上是常数，且这迫使刚性。我们还直接证明夹挤0≤Ric≤λ g迫使常数量曲率和径向平坦性，通过Petersen-Wylie刻画得到刚性结论。证明结合了加权截断论证和Ricci自同态的局部Codazzi对称性。

    🌐 English Abstract

    Let $(M^{2m},g,f,J)$ be a complete nonsteady gradient K\"ahler-Ricci soliton satisfying $\mathrm{Ric}+\nabla^2 f=\lambda g$, $\lambda\neq 0$. We prove that constant scalar curvature forces the soliton to be rigid. More precisely, the universal cover splits holomorphically and isometrically as $N^{2k}\times\mathbb{C}^{m-k}$, where $N^{2k}$ is K\"ahler-Einstein with $\mathrm{Ric}_{g_N}=\lambda g_N$. In the shrinking case the quotient is trivial; in the normalization $\lambda=1/2$ one has $R\equiv k$. The K\"ahler result rests on a Riemannian rigidity criterion. On a complete nonsteady gradient Ricci soliton, if $\mathcal{L}_{\nabla f}\mathrm{Ric}$ is nonnegative or nonpositive everywhere, then it vanishes and the soliton is rigid; no assumption on the scalar curvature is needed. Along the Ricci flow generated by the soliton, this means that a Ricci tensor that is monotone in time is constant in time, and that this forces rigidity. We also give a direct proof that the pinching $0\leq\mathrm{Ric}\leq\lambda g$ forces constant scalar curvature and radial flatness, yielding the rigidity conclusion through the Petersen-Wylie characterization. The proof combines a weighted cutoff argument with a partial Codazzi symmetry for the Ricci endomorphism.

    👥 Mauricio Godoy Molina, Marcos Salvai

    振子群是四维可解Lie群，是Heisenberg Lie群的扩张。我们将其呈现为R^3上标准接触次Riemannian结构的圆丛。我们在其上定义次Riemannian和次Lorentzian结构，显式描述其测地线并确定哪些是周期的。我们还研究了一个推广：设G是半单Lie群，K是紧子群，使得(G,K)是紧型或非紧型的对称对。设g和k分别是G和K的Lie代数。我们在g上考虑通常的幂零Lie群结构，中心为k，称为N(g,k)。与Ad(K)的适当半直积产生一个可解Lie群Osc(G,K)，它推广了振子群，也是二次的（即具有双不变度量；特别地，有一个典范的）。我们在其上定义非完整伪Riemannian结构并显式找到其测地线。在此过程中，我们获得了一个可能不仅仅是辅助的结果：Osc(G,K)的单参数子群公式和N(g,k)上具有标准左不变分布D的次Riemannian测地线公式。此外，当G/K是紧的且秩为一时，我们将Osc(G,K)实现为(N(g,k),D)的Stiefel丛，直到有限覆盖。

    🌐 English Abstract

    The oscillator groups are four-dimensional solvable Lie groups, extensions of the Heisenberg Lie group. We present them as circle bundles of the standard contact sub-Riemannian structure on $\mathbb{R}^{3}$. We define sub-Riemannian and sub-Lorentzian structures on them, describe their geodesics explicitly and determine which of them are periodic. We also study a generalization: Let $G$ be a semisimple Lie group and $K$ a compact subgroup such that $\left( G,K\right) $ is a symmetric pair of the compact or the noncompact type. Let $\mathfrak{g}$ and $% \mathfrak{k}$ be the Lie algebras of $G$ and $K$, respectively. We consider on $\mathfrak{g}$ the usual nilpotent Lie group structure with center $\mathfrak{k}$ and call it $N\left( \mathfrak{g},\mathfrak{k}\right)$. A suitable semidirect product with $\operatorname{Ad}\left( K\right) $ yields a solvable Lie group $\operatorname{Osc}\left( G,K\right) $, which generalizes the oscillator groups and is also quadratic (that is, it possesses bi-invariant metrics; in particular, a canonical one). We define nonholonomic pseudo-Riemannian structures on it and find their geodesics explicitly. In doing so, we obtain a result that may not be merely auxiliary: formulas for the monoparametric subgroups of $\operatorname{Osc}\left( G,K\right) $ and for the sub-Riemannian geodesics of $N\left( \mathfrak{g},\mathfrak{k}\right) $ with the standard left-invariant distribution $\mathcal{D}$. Moreover, when $G/K$ is compact with rank one, we realize $\operatorname{Osc}\left( G,K\right) $ as a Stiefel bundle of $\left( N\left( \mathfrak{g},\mathfrak{k}\right) ,\mathcal{D}\right) $, up to finite coverings.

    👥 Ryuichi Fukuoka, Anderson Macedo Setti

    📖 中文摘要

    设M是光滑流形，TM是其切丛。M的C^0-Finsler结构是连续函数F: TM → [0, ∞)，使得限制在每个切空间上是不对称范数。F的磨光平滑是一族Finsler结构F_ε: TM → [0, ∞)，由ε > 0参数化并使用标准磨光器构造，使得当ε → 0时F_ε在紧子集上一致收敛到F。在本工作中，我们构造了F的两种磨光平滑。第一种，我们假设F: TM → [0, ∞)是强凸的，即F限制在每个切空间上是强凸的。第二种，我们假设M=G是Lie群且F:TG → [0, ∞)是强凸且左不变的，在这种情况下，F_ε也是左不变的。在两种情况下，我们证明如果F是Finsler结构，则诸如基本张量、Chern、Cartan、Hashiguchi和Berwald联络以及(M, F_ε)的旗曲率等对象在紧子集上一致收敛到(M, F)的相应对象。

    🌐 English Abstract

    Let $M$ be a smooth manifold and $TM$ its tangent bundle. A $C^0$-Finsler structure of $M$ is a continuous function $F: TM \to [0, \infty)$ such that restricted to each tangent space is an asymmetric norm. A mollifier smoothing of $F$ is a family of Finsler structures $F_\varepsilon: TM \to [0, \infty)$, parameterized by $\varepsilon > 0$ and constructed using the standard mollifier, such that $F_\varepsilon \to F$ uniformly on compact subsets as $\varepsilon \to 0$. In this work, we construct two mollifier smoothings of $F$. In the first, we assume that $F: TM \to [0, \infty)$ is strongly convex, that is, that $F$ restricted to each tangent space is strongly convex. In the second, we assume that $M=G$ is a Lie group and that $F:TG \to [0, \infty)$ is strongly convex and left-invariant, and in this case, $F_\varepsilon$ is also left-invariant. In both cases, we prove that if $F$ is a Finsler structure, objects such as the fundamental tensor, the Chern, Cartan, Hashiguchi and Berwald connections, and the flag curvature of $(M, F_\varepsilon)$ converge uniformly on compact subsets to the corresponding objects of $(M, F)$ as $\varepsilon \to 0$.

    📖 中文摘要

    我们发展了到射影簇的拟映射的模墙穿越理论，允许边界系数和拟映射权重的独立变化。基于Hashizume和第一作者引入的拟映射的K-稳定性，我们在稳定、Calabi-Yau和对数Fano区域构造了射影模空间，以及墙穿越态射。一个核心构造是边界极化Calabi-Yau拟映射的模理论，它在数值平凡轨迹上保留一个丰极化，并允许与向稳定和对数Fano区域的适当扰动进行比较。稳定理论适用于任意亏格，而通过Calabi-Yau轨迹的比较涉及亏格零。对于度数一的边界除子，所得框架将加权稳定映射和拟映射与Hassett空间和P^1上加权点的GIT商联系起来。在 companion paper 中，我们应用此框架给出有理椭圆曲面的Miranda GIT紧化与八维球商的Baily-Borel紧化之间的模插值。

    🌐 English Abstract

    We develop a modular wall crossing theory for quasimaps to a projective variety, allowing independent variation of the boundary coefficients and the quasimap weight. Building on the K-stability of quasimaps introduced by Hashizume and the first author, we construct projective moduli spaces in the stable, Calabi--Yau, and log Fano regimes, together with wall crossing morphisms. A central construction is the moduli theory of boundary polarized Calabi--Yau quasimaps, which retains an ample polarization at the numerically trivial locus and allows comparison with suitable perturbations toward the stable and log Fano regions. The stable theory applies in arbitrary genus, while the comparisons through the Calabi--Yau locus concern genus zero. For degree-one boundary divisors, the resulting framework relates weighted stable maps and quasimaps to Hassett spaces and GIT quotients of weighted points on $\mathbb P^1$. In a companion paper, we apply this framework to give a modular interpolation between Miranda's GIT compactification of rational elliptic surfaces and the Baily--Borel compactification of an eight-dimensional ball quotient.

    Sharp commutator bounds on complex simple Lie algebras: the B\"ottcher--Wenzel inequality and the comass of the Cartan $3$-form
    👥 Daniel J. F. Fox
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 研究复单Lie代数上的交换子不等式。证明尖锐界等价于Cartan 3-形式余质量不增。包含并推广Böttcher-Wenzel和Bloch-Iserles不等式。几何上给出最大复截面曲率等于最大截面曲率。
    💡 证明了复单Lie代数上的尖锐交换子界，等价于Cartan 3-形式的余质量在复化下不增。
    📖 中文摘要

    对于具有紧共轭τ、Killing形式B和Hermitian形式H(x, y) = -B(x, τ y)的复单Lie代数g，证明H([x,y],[x,y]) ≤ (h^∨)^{-1}H(x,x)H(y,y)，其中h^∨是对偶Coxeter数，等号恰好对于τ-稳定长根子代数中H-正交对成立，这些子代数同构于sl(2, C)。等价地，Cartan 3-形式关于H的余质量等于其在紧实形式上的余质量：Cartan 3-形式的谱范数在复化下不增加。对于Dynkin指数为ℓ的表示的像上的Frobenius范数，最优常数是2/ℓ；这包含了Böttcher-Wenzel和Bloch-Iserles不等式，将后者推广到复斜对称矩阵，并对类型F_4、E_6、E_7和E_8的例外Lie代数大幅改进。证明使用g的Nahm代数与由τ诱导的共轭：相关函数的临界点对应于τ-扭曲幂等元，其Hessian由τ-扭曲乘法算子控制，其与乘以i的反对易性将最大值处的一侧二阶条件转化为两侧谱界。Dynkin指数为j的同构于sl(2, C)的子代数给出临界值为1/(jh^∨)的临界点，计算了其Morse指数，最大点集是非退化临界流形。几何上，紧单Lie群的最大复截面曲率等于其最大截面曲率。

    🌐 English Abstract

    For a complex simple Lie algebra $\mathfrak{g}$ with compact conjugation $\tau$, Killing form $B$, and Hermitian form $H(x, y) = -B(x, \tau y)$, it is shown that $H([x,y],[x,y]) \leq (h^{\vee})^{-1}H(x,x)H(y,y)$, where $h^{\vee}$ is the dual Coxeter number, with equality exactly for $H$-orthogonal pairs in $\tau$-stable long-root subalgebras isomorphic to $\mathfrak{sl}(2, \mathbb{C})$. Equivalently, the comass of the Cartan $3$-form with respect to $H$ equals its comass on the compact real form: the spectral norm of the Cartan $3$-form does not increase under complexification. For the Frobenius norm on the image of a representation of Dynkin index $\ell$ the optimal constant is $2/\ell$; this contains the B\"ottcher--Wenzel and Bloch--Iserles inequalities, extends the latter to complex skew-symmetric matrices, and improves them substantially for the exceptional Lie algebras of types $F_{4}$, $E_{6}$, $E_{7}$, and $E_{8}$. The proof uses the Nahm algebra of $\mathfrak{g}$ with the conjugation induced by $\tau$: critical points of the relevant function correspond to $\tau$-twisted idempotents, and its Hessian is governed by $\tau$-twisted multiplication operators, whose anticommutation with multiplication by $i$ turns the one-sided second order condition at a maximum into a two-sided spectral bound. Subalgebras isomorphic to $\mathfrak{sl}(2, \mathbb{C})$ of Dynkin index $j$ give critical points with critical value $1/(jh^{\vee})$, whose Morse indices are computed, and the set of maximum points is a nondegenerate critical manifold. Geometrically, the maximal complex sectional curvature of a compact simple Lie group equals its maximal sectional curvature.

    Atiyah classes and ellipticity of Oka manifolds
    👥 Yuta Kusakabe, Shin-ichi Matsumura
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 研究Oka流形的椭圆性。证明弱伪凸Oka流形是Gromov椭圆的。建立局部喷雾的上同调构造。给出非椭圆例子并讨论椭圆性的保持性。
    💡 证明了每个具有正线丛的弱伪凸Oka流形是Gromov椭圆的，解决了Gromov的椭圆性猜想。
    📖 中文摘要

    我们证明每个具有正线丛的弱伪凸Oka流形是Gromov意义下椭圆的，从而证明了Gromov的椭圆性猜想。证明建立了局部喷雾的上同调构造。给定丛态射φ: E → T_X，我们引入E上的二次φ-向量场，其流产生具有纤维导数φ的局部喷雾。它们的存在性由E的对称化φ-Atiyah类的消失刻画。这给出了每个具有正线丛的弱伪凸流形上的局部支配喷雾，无需任何Oka假设。正性假设即使对于局部存在性也是本质的：代数维数为零的复环面在单点处的爆破，以及代数维数为零的Kummer曲面，不允许局部支配喷雾。环面例子给出了非椭圆的紧Kähler Oka流形，并表明椭圆性在爆破下不保持，且在紧Oka流形的全纯族中既不是开的也不是闭的。结合Xie和Zhao的工作，Kummer例子产生非椭圆的Oka K3曲面。在额外的Oka假设下，我们将上述构造的局部支配喷雾全局化。证明结合了Oka逼近和基于加权L^2估计的粘合论证。

    🌐 English Abstract

    We prove that every weakly pseudoconvex Oka manifold admitting a positive line bundle is elliptic in the sense of Gromov, thereby proving Gromov's ellipticity conjecture. The proof establishes a cohomological construction of local sprays. Given a bundle morphism $\varphi\colon E\to T_X$, we introduce quadratic $\varphi$-vector fields on $E$ whose flows yield local sprays with fibre derivative $\varphi$. Their existence is characterized by the vanishing of the symmetrized $\varphi$-Atiyah class of $E$. This gives a local dominating spray on every weakly pseudoconvex manifold admitting a positive line bundle, without any Oka assumption. The positivity hypothesis is essential even for local existence: blow-ups at a single point of complex tori of algebraic dimension zero, and Kummer surfaces of algebraic dimension zero, admit no local dominating spray. The torus examples give compact K\"ahler Oka manifolds that are not elliptic and show that ellipticity is not preserved under blowing up, and that it is neither open nor closed in holomorphic families of compact Oka manifolds. Combined with the work of Xie and Zhao, the Kummer examples yield Oka K3 surfaces that are not elliptic. Under the additional Oka assumption, we globalize the local dominating sprays constructed above. The proof combines Oka approximation with a gluing argument based on weighted $L^2$ estimates for the $\bar\partial$-equation.

    👉 https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12175

    🌐 English Abstract

    Given independent and identically distributed samples samples from a probability distribution in a potentially high-dimensional real space, we study the problem of testing whether the distribution is concentrated near a real algebraic complete intersection of prescribed dimension, bounded degree, and bounded condition number. We design an explicit and effective learning procedure which either certifies the nonexistence of such a manifold, up to a controlled relaxation of the approximation threshold, or returns a candidate regression manifold with controlled geometric complexity. Equivalently, the procedure tests the manifold hypothesis within this hypothesis class. The proposed procedure relies on quantitative geometric estimates for regular polynomial systems, which lead to a tractable auxiliary optimization problem. We then develop a data-driven algorithm to solve this auxiliary optimization problem, establishing explicit bounds on its sample and arithmetic complexity.

    📖 中文摘要

    设 π:(X,L)→D* 是 P2 中的费马三次曲线族。对于每个 k≥1，我们根据 H0(P2,O_P2(l)) 中截面的限制，为 H0(X,Lk) 构造了一个显式的赋值独立基。作为结果，我们得到了规范热带theta函数和由 H0(X,Lk) 的任意赋值独立基定义的规范成本函数的显式公式。我们证明规范热带theta函数可以描述为实Monge-Ampère算子的基本解，规范成本函数可以用本质骨架上的Hessian结构的单值性内蕴描述。值得注意的是，规范热带theta函数不同于由单项式基诱导的函数，规范成本函数不同于由环境射影空间诱导的函数。

    🌐 English Abstract

    Let $\pi:(X,L)\rightarrow \mathbb D^*$ be the Fermat family of cubic curves in $\mathbb P^2$. For each $k\geq 1$, we construct an explicit valuatively independent basis for $H^0(X,L^k)$ in terms of restrictions of sections in $H^0(\mathbb P^2,\mathcal O_\mathbb P^2(l))$. As a consequence, we get an explicit formula for the canonical tropical theta functions and the canonical cost function defined by \emph{any} valuatively independent bases of $H^0(X,L^k)$. We show that the canonical tropical theta functions can be described as fundamental solutions to a real Monge-Amp\`ere operator and the canonical cost function can be described intrinsically in terms of the monodromy of a Hessian structure on the essential skeleton. Notably, the canonical tropical theta functions differ from the ones induced by a monomial basis and the canonical cost function differs from the one induced by the ambient projective space.

    🌐 English Abstract

    We address the recently introduced notions of generalized principal bundle and generalized principal connection by keeping track of global geometric properties through local coordinate transformation laws. This approach leads us to introduce generalized principal bundle coordinates and to find their transformation laws. Besides, we show that any Lie group fiber bundle (and hence, in particular, any vector bundle) is a generalized principal bundle and we give a proof of the fact that any Lie group fiber bundle with connected typical fiber is an associated bundle to a suitable principal bundle. Moreover, we present a direct way to characterize Lie group fiber bundle connections and generalized principal connections in terms of horizontal lifts and of local conditions. Finally, we recover in our setting some already known results, including that generalized principal connections are associated only to Lie group fiber bundle connections and that they reduce to usual principal connections on standard principal bundles. Our results are needed in order to understand how generalized principal connections might fit in the fiber bundle treatment of classical field theories, aiming towards a notion of generalized gauge theory.

We emphasize Calabi-Yau information manifolds which profane theoretical guarantees via an ill-curvature-conditioned landscape. We focus on Calabi-Yau metrics specifically in a non-compact setting with a global potential, so defined geometrically rather than invoking the topological requirements of the Calabi conjecture. We isolate the Calabi-Yau case in a theoretical setting, and we counteract the corrupted geometries under regularization. Moreover, it has been discovered that negative curvature subverts the loss landscape, specifically sectional curvature, so we expand on this and draw interconnections to negative-definite Ricci curvature. Our arguments primarily exist via geometric analysis, although we establish roots in deep learning theory such as through asymptotics at initialization and connections through failure modes of neural network guarantees under vanishing and negative Ricci curvature.

    对于豪斯多夫局部准凸阿贝尔群G，设C(G)是其底层群上所有具有与G相同连续特征的豪斯多夫局部准凸群拓扑的偏序集。我们证明，只要G非预紧，C(G)包含(Pow(cont),⊆)的序同构副本，其中cont=2^{aleph_0}。嵌入取值在Bohr拓扑和原始拓扑之间。因此，每个无限离散阿贝尔群D满足|C(D)|=width C(D)=2^{2^{|D|}}。局部紧阿贝尔群的离散约化然后给出所有此类群的精确基数和宽度公式；特别地，对于每个非紧σ-紧局部紧阿贝尔群，这两个不变量都等于2^{cont}。这些结果回答了L. Außenhofer和D. Dikranjan在[AD20]中提出的问题6.1--6.3和6.5--6.7，以及问题6.4的局部紧情况。嵌入也适用于非紧完备可度量局部准凸群。一个具有唯一相容拓扑的非紧预紧核群表明，在核设置中仅非紧性是不够的。最后，R^N的相容偏序集不与任何离散阿贝尔群的序同构。

    🌐 English Abstract

    For a Hausdorff locally quasi-convex abelian group $G$, let $\C(G)$ be the poset of all Hausdorff locally quasi-convex group topologies on its underlying group having the same continuous characters as $G$. We prove that, whenever $G$ is non-precompact, $\C(G)$ contains an order-isomorphic copy of $(\Pow(\cont),\subseteq)$, where $\cont=2^{\aleph_0}$. The embedding takes values between the Bohr topology and the original topology. Consequently, every infinite discrete abelian group $D$ satisfies $|\C(D)|=\width\C(D)=2^{2^{|D|}}$. The discrete reduction for locally compact abelian groups then yields exact cardinality and width formulas for all such groups; in particular, both invariants equal $2^{\cont}$ for every non-compact $\sigma$-compact locally compact abelian group. These results answer Questions 6.1--6.3 and 6.5--6.7, and the locally compact case of Question 6.4, posed by L.~Au{\ss}enhofer and D.~Dikranjan in \cite{AD20}. The embedding also applies to non-compact complete metrizable locally quasi-convex groups. A non-compact precompact nuclear group with a unique compatible topology shows that non-compactness alone does not suffice in the nuclear setting. Finally, the compatible poset of $\R^{\N}$ is not order-isomorphic to that of any discrete abelian group.

    我们引入了一种系统的方法，利用任何范畴上的子对象函子来建立广义拓扑和测度论概念及推理的框架。具体地，我们发展了两种相互关联的演算：1. 从微小性和稠密性概念导出的四重演算，它推广了拓扑稠密性和测度论可忽略性等；2. 推广拓扑和测度论推理过程的二重演算。所得理论的特点是它具有范畴间特性：其定义不仅可以应用于描述任何范畴中子对象的微小性和稠密性，并根据它们如何处理这些来分类态射，还可以分类函子和自然变换如何变换它们，有点像极限理论，但具有简单直观的概念意义，适用于每个化身。我们提供了来自数学许多领域的这些概念的例子，并概述了它们在范畴论中随着我们添加假设而展开的过程，这些假设使它们更接近经典设置，我们通过推广开集和闭子集的拓扑对偶性并概述范畴测度论的可能框架来完成。这种相互作用的核心是子对象2-函子和向上有界预序的2-范畴，因此在文本的最后部分我们提供了它们功能的研究。具体地，我们推导了该2-函子和2-范畴的表示定理，以及关于如何将这些定理推广到n-范畴的猜想。

    🌐 English Abstract

    We introduce a systematic way to use the subobject functor on any category to set up an framework for generalized topological and measure-theoretic notions and inferences. Concretely, we develop two connected calculi: 1. a fourfold calculus derived from notions of smallness and density that generalizes, among other things, topological density and measure-theoretic negligibility, 2. a twofold calculus that generalizes topological and measure-theoretic inference procedures. The resulting theory is distinguished in that it is inter-categorical in character: its definitions can be applied not just to describe smallness and density of subobjects in any category and classify morphisms by how they handle them, but also functors and natural transformations by how they transform them, somewhat like the theory of limits but with a simple and intuitive conceptual meaning that applies to each incarnation. We provide examples of these notions from many areas of mathematics and outline their unfolding throughout category theory as we add assumptions that bring them closer to classical settings, which we complete by generalizing the topological duality of open and closed subsets and outlining a possible framework for a categorical measure theory. Central to this interaction is the subobject 2-functor and the 2-category of upward-bounded preorders, so in the last part of the text we provide a study of their function. Concretely we derive representation theorems for this 2-functor and 2-category and a conjecture about how to generalize these theorems to n-categories.

    👉 https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10930

This plays an important role in the work of Putman-Wieland on the virtual first Betti number of $\mathop{Mod}_{g,n}$.     🌐 English Abstract

In the complex-algebraic category we show that equality holds exactly when the arrangement is a maximising curve, hence free with prescribed exponents. We then introduce divisible and odd combinatorial conic-line arrangements and obtain inequalities and congruences for the multiplicity sequence of their resolutions, via cyclic branched covers, the G-signature theorem, spin structures, and Furuta's 10/8-theorem.

    • 研究 veering 三角剖分的钻取算法及其应用。
    • 构造 veering 父结构，组合化钻取伪 Anosov 流的闭轨。
    • 实现算法构造 Birkhoff 截面和几何父结构。
    • 为 Ghys 猜想提供计算证据，推动 Anosov 流分类。
    💡 开发钻取 veering 三角剖分的算法，构造 veering 父结构，应用于伪 Anosov 流的 Birkhoff 截面和几何父结构。
    📖 中文摘要

    我们开发了一种算法，输入 veering 三角剖分 V 和 V 的流循环 c，输出 V 的 veering 父结构 V_c。构造 veering 父结构将传递伪 Anosov 流的闭轨钻取组合化。该算法依赖于对 V 关联的织机空间结构的局部近似，以进行 Agol-Guéritaud 构造的某种修改。我们使用钻取算法的实现来构造非分层 veering 三角剖分的分层父结构（从而传递伪 Anosov 流的 Birkhoff 截面）和非几何 veering 三角剖分的几何父结构。我们还发现（钻取后）伪 Anosov 流的闭轨不环境同痕于其自由同伦类中的测地线，并证明某些 veering 三角剖分编码几乎轨道等价的流。特别地，我们为 Ghys 的猜想提供了新的计算证据，该猜想断言所有具有可定向不变叶状结构的传递 Anosov 流是几乎轨道等价的。

    🌐 English Abstract

    We develop an algorithm that takes as input a veering triangulation V and a flow cycle c of V, and outputs a veering parent V_c of V. Constructing veering parents combinatorializes drilling out closed orbits of transitive pseudo-Anosov flows. The algorithm relies on locally approximating the structure of the loom space associated to V in sufficient detail to carry out a certain modification of the Agol-Gu\'eritaud construction. We use an implementation of the drilling algorithm to construct layered parents of non-layered veering triangulations (and hence Birkhoff sections for transitive pseudo-Anosov flows) and geometric parents of non-geometric veering triangulations. We also find closed orbits of (drilled) pseudo-Anosov flows that are not ambiently isotopic to the geodesics in their free homotopy classes, and show that certain veering triangulations encode almost orbit equivalent flows. In particular, we provide new computational evidence for a conjecture of Ghys asserting that all transitive Anosov flows with orientable invariant foliations are almost orbit equivalent.

    👉 https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12302
  12. [2610.12347] 同调球面的图着色
    Coloring graphs of homologous spheres
    👥 Edgar A. Bering IV, Samantha Ford, Aarav Ghai, Maxwell Lyon, Om Shah
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 研究 M_r 球面图中本原同调类诱导的子图着色。
    • 证明该图唯一 (r-1)-可着色。
    • 得到 Torelli 子群到对称群的非平凡同态。
    • 找到映射类群的新生成集，类比曲线图理论。
    💡 研究 M_r 的球面图中由单个本原同调类诱导的子图，证明其唯一 (r-1)-可着色，并得到 Torelli 子群到对称群的非平凡同态。
    📖 中文摘要

    曲面的曲线图是顶点为曲面上本质闭曲线的同痕类、边连接有不相交代表的顶点的图。曲线图在曲面映射类群理论中起核心作用。Gaster、Greene 和 Vlamis 证明了由单个本原同调类中的曲线诱导的曲线图子图是唯一有限可着色的。在曲面映射类群与自由群外自同构理论的类比中，M_r（r 个 S_1×S_2 的连通和）的球面图起类似作用。受 Gaster、Greene 和 Vlamis 结果的启发，我们研究 S_v(M_r)，即由表示单个本原同调类 [v]∈H_2(M_r;Z) 的球面诱导的 M_r 球面图的子图。我们证明该图是唯一 (r-1)-可着色的。作为推论，当 r≥3 时，我们得到从 Torelli 子群 IO_r≤Out(F_r) 到 r-1 个符号的对称群的非平凡同态；这平行于 Gaster、Greene 和 Vlamis 的着色与 Chillingworth 同态的联系。在证明过程中，我们找到了删除球面的 M_r 映射类群的新的更小生成集。

    🌐 English Abstract

    The curve graph of a surface is the graph whose vertices are isotopy classes of essential, closed curves on the surface. Edges join vertices with disjoint representatives. The curve graph plays a central role in the theory of mapping class groups of surfaces. Gaster, Greene, and Vlamis have shown that the subgraph of the curve graph induced by curves in a single primitive homology class is uniquely and finitely colorable. In the analogy between the theory of mapping class groups of surfaces and outer automorphisms of free groups, the sphere graph of $M_r$, a connect sum of $r$ copies of $S_1 \times S_2$ plays an analogous role in the theory. Motivated by Gaster, Green, and Vlamis' result, we investigate $\mathcal{S}_v(M_r)$, the subgraph of the sphere graph of $M_r$ induced by spheres representing a single primitive homology class $[v] \in H_2(M_r;\mathbb{Z})$. We show that this graph is uniquely $r-1$ colorable. As a corollary, with $r\ge 3$ we obtain a nontrivial homomorphism from the Torelli subgroup $IO_r \le \operatorname{Out}(F_r)$ to the symmetric group on $r-1$ symbols; this parallels Gaster, Greene, and Vlamis' connection between their coloring and the Chillingworth homomorphism. In the course of our proof we find new smaller generating sets for mapping class groups of $M_r$ with spheres deleted.

    👉 https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12347

  15. [2610.11243] 双接触塞与动力双曲性
    Bi-contact plugs and dynamical hyperbolicity
    👥 Surena Hozoori
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 发展三维双接触塞理论构造 Anosov 流。
    • 证明强横截粘合映射可粘合接触形式。
    • 回答 Béguin-Bonatti-Yu 问题并推广到部分双曲流。
    • 应用于分类射影 Anosov 流和构造 Liouville 域。
    💡 发展三维双接触塞理论，构造 Anosov 流和结构稳定流，证明强横截粘合映射可粘合接触形式。
    📖 中文摘要

    我们发展三维双接触塞理论以构造 Anosov 流或更一般的结构稳定非奇异流。对于具有可定向填充 Morse-Smale 边界叶状结构的横截可定向双曲塞，我们证明每个强横截粘合映射可通过强横截映射同痕，以识别强适应接触形式（差一个符号）。所得流是双曲的，当商闭时是 Anosov 的。这在所述可定向假设下回答了 Béguin-Bonatti-Yu 的问题。结果也推广到粘合非奇异部分双曲流。通过反射的 Anosov 完备化将每个这样的双曲塞嵌入其有向双倍上的 Anosov 流。进一步应用包括分类无鞍周期轨道的结构一般横截有向射影 Anosov 流、具有指定鞍数的 Morse-Smale 例子、推广 Bonatti-Bowden-Potrie 的构造以将（吸引）双曲塞嵌入具有相同拓扑熵的（部分双曲）射影 Anosov 流，以及在双倍和环面丛上具有受控不变环面动力学的构造。最后，每个余定向部分双曲双接触塞在其加厚上允许 Liouville 结构，Liouville 轨迹投影到正重参数化流线。排斥塞产生四维 Liouville 域，通常具有混沌骨架。

    🌐 English Abstract

    We develop a theory of bi-contact plugs in dimension three to construct Anosov flows or, more generally, structurally stable nonsingular flows. For a transversely orientable hyperbolic plug with orientable filling Morse-Smale boundary laminations, we prove that every strongly transverse gluing map can be isotoped through strongly transverse maps to identify a strongly adapted contact form up to sign. The resulting flow is hyperbolic and is Anosov when the quotient is closed. This answers a question of B\'eguin-Bonatti-Yu under the stated orientability assumptions. The result also extends to gluing nonsingular partially hyperbolic flows. Anosov completion by reflection embeds each such hyperbolic plug in an Anosov flow on its oriented double. Further applications include a classification of structurally generic transversely oriented projectively Anosov flows without saddle periodic orbits, Morse-Smale examples with prescribed saddle count, a generalization of the construction of Bonatti-Bowden-Potrie to give embeddings of (attracting) hyperbolic plugs into (partially hyperbolic) projectively Anosov flows with the same topological entropy, and constructions on doubles and torus bundles with controlled invariant torus dynamics. Finally, every cooriented partially hyperbolic bi-contact plug admits a Liouville structure on its thickening, with Liouville trajectories projecting to positively reparametrized flow lines. Repelling plugs yield four-dimensional Liouville domains, often with chaotic skeletons.

    👉 https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11243

    📖 中文摘要

    设 Γ<PSL_2(R) 是无挠、非初等、有限生成且含抛物元素的 Fuchsian 群，ρ:Γ→G 是到连通分裂半单实代数群的保型、Zariski 稠密、正表示。我们证明 ρ(Γ)\G/M 上 Bowen-Margulis-Sullivan 测度的定量局部混合，其中 M 是兼容极大紧子群中极大分裂环面的中心化子。对于 ρ(Γ) 的极限锥内部的每个 u，沿 exp(tu) 的光滑紧支函数的关联，经 t^{(rank_R G-1)/2} 归一化后，允许 t^{-1} 的整数幂的完全渐近展开。我们还得到横向位移的一致高斯渐近，余项可积。我们推导了 L^2(ρ(Γ)\G) 上准正则表示的矩阵系数和黎曼球中轨道计数的渐近。更一般地，这些结论对满足外围根平衡条件的 Zariski 稠密、相对 Borel Anosov 表示成立。这包括到秩至多三的分裂单群的相对 Borel Anosov 表示和保型几何有限实双曲表示的自 join。为证明这些结果，我们在 Furstenberg 边界上 ρ(Γ) 的极限集中发展内蕴尖点几何，并用其构造可数 Markov 编码。我们建立无界向量值返回余循环的指数矩界和一致扭曲估计。利用外围 Cartan 增长和 Zariski 稠密性，我们建立谱分析所需的定量非可积性。

    🌐 English Abstract

    Let $\Gamma<\operatorname{PSL}_2(\mathbb R)$ be a torsion-free, non-elementary, finitely generated Fuchsian group with parabolic elements, and let $\rho:\Gamma\to G$ be a type-preserving, Zariski dense, positive representation into a connected split semisimple real algebraic group. We prove quantitative local mixing for Bowen--Margulis--Sullivan measures on $\rho(\Gamma)\backslash G/M$, where $M$ is the centralizer of a maximal split torus in a compatible maximal compact subgroup. For every $u$ in the interior of the limit cone of $\rho(\Gamma)$, correlations of smooth compactly supported functions along $\exp(tu)$, normalized by $t^{(\operatorname{rank}_{\mathbb R}G-1)/2}$, admit a complete asymptotic expansion in integer powers of $t^{-1}$. We also obtain a uniform Gaussian asymptotic for transverse displacements, with an integrable remainder. We deduce asymptotics for matrix coefficients of the quasi-regular representation on $L^2(\rho(\Gamma)\backslash G)$ and for orbital counting in Riemannian balls. More generally, these conclusions hold for Zariski dense, relatively Borel Anosov representations of $\Gamma$ satisfying a peripheral root-balance condition. This includes relatively Borel Anosov representations into split simple groups of rank at most three and self-joinings of type-preserving geometrically finite real hyperbolic representations. To prove these results, we develop an intrinsic cusp geometry on the limit set of $\rho(\Gamma)$ in the Furstenberg boundary and use it to construct a countable Markov coding. We establish exponential moment bounds and uniform distortion estimates for the unbounded vector-valued return cocycle. Using peripheral Cartan growth and Zariski density, we establish the quantitative non-integrability needed for the spectral analysis.

  3. [2610.10553] Conway-Wales 格与 Rudvalis 和 Tits 群
    The Conway-Wales lattice and the Rudvalis and Tits groups
    👥 Gerald Höhn
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 从 Z_4 自对偶偶码构造 Conway-Wales 格。
    • 确定完整高斯等距群并得到 Rudvalis 单群。
    • 交叉稳定化子导子群给出 Tits 单群。
    • 在格内完成构造和单性证明，无需分类定理。
    💡 从 Z_4 上的自对偶偶码构造 Conway-Wales 格，确定其等距群，并得到 Rudvalis 和 Tits 单群。
    📖 中文摘要

    我们从 Z_4 上的自对偶偶码 C 构造 Conway-Wales 格，其二进制层交织 Hamming 码 H_7 和 H_8。显式带符号对称性提升二进制剩余的每个自同构，完整带符号码群是高斯格的坐标框架稳定化子。嵌入的缩放自对偶子格中的整反射提供额外对称性，从中恢复经典框架交换。计数内蕴定义的框架确定完整高斯等距群，阶为 583,704,576,000。其模四个高斯标量的商是 Rudvalis 单群，交叉稳定化子的导子群是阶为 17,971,200 的 Tits 单群。构造、自同构群确定和两个单性证明均在格内进行，不假设已知群阶或使用识别或分类定理。

    🌐 English Abstract

    We construct the Conway-Wales lattice from a self-dual even code $C$ over $\mathbb{Z}_4$ whose binary layers interweave the Hamming codes $\mathcal{H}_7$ and $\mathcal{H}_8$. Explicit signed symmetries lift every automorphism of the binary residue, and the full signed code group is the coordinate-frame stabilizer of the Gaussian lattice. An integral reflection in an embedded scaled self-dual sublattice supplies an additional symmetry, from which the classical frame exchange is recovered. Counting intrinsically defined frames determines the full Gaussian isometry group, of order $583\,704\,576\,000$. Its quotient by the four Gaussian scalars is the Rudvalis simple group, and the derived subgroup of a cross stabilizer is the Tits simple group of order $17\,971\,200$. The construction, automorphism-group determination, and both simplicity proofs are carried out within the lattice, without assuming the known group orders or using a recognition or classification theorem.

    • 构造尖锐障碍，证明 Smith 猜想。
    💡 证明群可从每个生成子集恢复当且仅当群平凡或阶为 2 或 3 的循环群，证明 Smith 猜想。
    📖 中文摘要

    对于包含恒等元的群的对称子集，其泛局部表示记录子集内可见的乘法关系。我们证明群可从每个这样的生成子集恢复当且仅当它是平凡的或阶为 2 或 3 的循环群，如 P. A. Smith 所猜想。主要步骤是一个精确完备化定理：到目标群的局部映射总是可扩张到生成域当且仅当每个目标元素有平方根。通过将任意群嵌入到平方映射为满射的群中，我们推出每个部分局部表示嵌入到生成表示中。这给出子群继承性并将分类约化为有限生成。我们还在一个固定可数亚交换目标上构造了至多五个元素的域上的障碍。即使允许目标变化，这个界也是尖锐的。

    🌐 English Abstract

    For a symmetric subset of a group containing the identity, its universal local presentation records the multiplication relations visible within the subset. We prove that a group is recovered from every such generating subset if and only if it is trivial or cyclic of order two or three, as conjectured by P. A. Smith. The main step is an exact completion theorem: local maps into a target group always extend to generating domains if and only if every target element has a square root. By embedding an arbitrary group in a group with surjective squaring, we deduce that every partial local presentation embeds in a generating one. This gives subgroup inheritance and reduces the classification to finite generation. We also construct obstructions in one fixed countable metabelian target on domains of at most five elements. This bound is sharp even when the target is allowed to vary.

    👉 https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10586

    👥 Pratyush Mishra
    🤖 AI 总结

    • 发展两个新框架，实现为对称群直积子群。
    • 引入标记围长准则，研究围长与投射有限完备化。
    • 给出线性群同余实现结果并提出开放问题。
    💡 发展构造具有无限围长、无群律且无非阿贝尔自由子群的有限生成剩余有限群的新框架。
    📖 中文摘要

    我们发展两个新的一般框架，用于构造具有无限围长、不满足非平凡群律且不包含非阿贝尔自由子群的有限生成剩余有限群。虽然这样的群很少见——已知例子包括第一 Grigorchuk 群、Thompson 群 F 和某些迭代圈积——我们的方法提供了更显式的构造，实现为对称群直积的子群。我们研究群的围长与其有限商围长的关系，引入「标记」围长准则，并证明其在剩余有限群类中是无限围长的充要条件。此外，我们探讨围长与投射有限完备化的联系，并给出线性群中无限围长的同余实现结果。最后，我们提出几个自然产生的问题。

    🌐 English Abstract

    We develop two new general frameworks for constructing finitely generated residually finite groups that possess infinite girth, satisfy no non-trivial group law, and contain no non-abelian free subgroups. While such groups are rare--with known examples including the first Grigorchuk group, Thompson's group F, and certain iterated wreath products--our approach provides a more explicit construction of such groups realized as subgroups of direct products of symmetric groups. We investigate the relationship between the girth of a group and that of its finite quotients, leading to the introduction of a "marked" girth criterion, which we establish as a necessary and sufficient condition for infinite girth within the class of residually finite groups. Furthermore, we explore the connection between girth and profinite completions and provide a congruence realization result for infinite girth in linear groups. We conclude by posing several open questions that arise naturally from our results.

  6. [2610.11092] 几乎所有有限群都是类二的 2-群
    Almost all finite groups are $2$-groups of class two
    👥 Ning Su, Binzhou Xia
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 研究有限群的计数分布。
    • 证明几乎所有有限群是类二的 2-群。
    • 给出 p-群计数的精确公式和误差估计。
    • 改进了经典对数估计并描述生成元数分布。
    💡 证明几乎所有有限群都是幂零类二的 2-群，并给出 p-群计数的精确公式和误差估计。
    📖 中文摘要

    我们证明当按同构计数阶至多 x 的群且 x→∞ 时，几乎所有有限群都是幂零类二的 2-群。更精确地，对 2^m≤x<2^{m+1} 一致地，除 O(2^{-cm^2}) 比例外，所有群阶为 2^m 且类二，其中 c>0 为绝对常数。对每个固定素数 p，我们给出阶为 p^m 的群数的公式，相对误差 O(p^{-m/3}) 当 m→∞。这改进了 p-群数的经典对数估计。对每个固定素数 p 和充分大的 m，我们还证明阶为 p^m 的群中除 p^{-m^2/300} 比例外，都有中心初等阿贝尔 Frattini 子群。对固定 p，阶为 p^m 的群中最小生成元数的分布渐近支撑在一个或两个相邻值上，显式概率取决于 m 模 3。

    🌐 English Abstract

    We prove that almost all finite groups are $2$-groups of nilpotency class two, when groups of order at most $x$ are counted up to isomorphism and $x\to\infty$. More precisely, uniformly for $2^m\leq x<2^{m+1}$, all but an $O(2^{-cm^2})$ proportion have order $2^m$ and class two, for some absolute constant $c>0$. For each fixed prime $p$, we give a formula for the number of groups of order $p^m$, with relative error $O(p^{-m/3})$ as $m\to\infty$. This refines the classical logarithmic estimates for the number of $p$-groups. For every fixed prime $p$ and sufficiently large $m$, we also prove that all but a $p^{-m^2/300}$ proportion of groups of order $p^m$ have central elementary abelian Frattini subgroup. For fixed $p$, the distribution of the minimum number of generators among groups of order $p^m$ is asymptotically supported on one or two adjacent values, with explicit probabilities depending on $m$ modulo three.

    👉 https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11092

    👥 Ville Salo
    🤖 AI 总结

    • 得到 Whitehead 问题和群论共轭的不可判定性。
    • 推广到 Cantor 空间同胚群的有限生成超群。
    💡 证明 Brin-Thompson 群 nV 中元素的拓扑共轭不可判定，并得到 Whitehead 问题和群论共轭的不可判定性。
    📖 中文摘要

    我们证明对每个 n≥2，Brin-Thompson 群 nV 中元素的拓扑共轭是不可判定的。从图灵机 T，归约产生 nV 的两个元素，若 T 在空带上停机，则它们在 nV 中由一个对合共轭。若 T 在空带上不停机，则两个群元素的拓扑动力学通过它们对（芽）非周期点集的极小系统的访问来区分。在这些群中，以及 Cantor 空间同胚群内的任何有限生成超群中，我们还得到 Whitehead 问题（处于同一自同构轨道的问题）的不可判定性和群论共轭的不可判定性。

    🌐 English Abstract

    We prove that topological conjugacy of elements of the Brin--Thompson group $nV$ is undecidable for every $n\geq2$. From a Turing machine $T$, the reduction produces two elements of $nV$, which are conjugate by an involution in $nV$ if $T$ halts on the empty tape. If $T$ does not halt on the empty tape, the topological dynamics of the two group elements are distinguished by their visits to minimal subsystems of the set of (germ-)aperiodic points. In these groups, and any finitely-generated supergroups inside the homeomorphism group of Cantor space, we also obtain undecidability of the Whitehead problem (the problem of being in the same automorphism orbit), and undecidability of group-theoretic conjugacy.

    👉 https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11124

    👥 Edgar Alcalá-Arroyo, Alonso Castillo-Ramirez, Maria G. Magaña-Chávez, Luguis de los Santos Baños
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 研究惰性元胞自动机的幂等性问题。
    • 引入相容集概念，给出非幂等的必要条件和充分条件。
    • 在阿贝尔群上，相容性完全刻画非幂等性。
    • 构造二面体群上的反例，说明交换性假设不可去。
    💡 引入相容集概念，给出惰性元胞自动机非幂等的必要条件和充分条件，并刻画阿贝尔群情形。
    📖 中文摘要

    惰性元胞自动机 τ:A^G→A^G 由包含群单位的有限邻域 S⊆G、模式 p∈A^S 和写入符号 a∈A∖{p(e)} 确定：其局部规则当邻域模式等于 p 时，将中心符号改为 a。对于 p 取值 a 的非空位置集 T，我们引入 p 沿右平移 St（t∈T）的两个相容性条件。我们证明相容集的存在性对 τ 在每个群上非幂等是必要的，并且当 p 取值 a 的每个位置与 p 取值 p(e) 的每个位置交换时也是充分的。特别地，相容性刻画了阿贝尔群上的非幂等性，将 SS={st:s,t∈S} 上模式的搜索替换为 p 取值 a 的位置集的搜索。最后，对每个 n≥4 的二面体群 D_n，我们构造一个幂等惰性元胞自动机，它允许相容集，表明交换性假设不能简单去掉。

    🌐 English Abstract

    A lazy cellular automaton $\tau:A^G\to A^G$ is determined by a finite neighborhood $S\subseteq G$ containing the group identity, a pattern $p\in A^S$, and a writing symbol $a\in A\setminus\{p(e)\}$: its local rule changes the central symbol to $a$ precisely when the neighborhood pattern equals $p$. For a nonempty set $T$ of positions where $p$ takes the value $a$, we introduce two compatibility conditions on $p$ along the right translates $St$, $t\in T$. We show that the existence of a compatible set is necessary for $\tau$ to be non-idempotent over every group, and that it is also sufficient whenever every position where $p$ takes the value $a$ commutes with every position where $p$ takes the value $p(e)$. In particular, compatibility characterizes non-idempotence over abelian groups, replacing a search over patterns on $SS=\{st:s,t\in S\}$ by a search over sets of positions where $p$ takes the value $a$. Finally, for every dihedral group $D_n$ with $n\geq 4$, we construct an idempotent lazy cellular automaton that admits a compatible set, showing that the commutation hypothesis cannot simply be dropped.

    📖 中文摘要

    设 S 表示 Z 的 k 元子集。设 G 是一个大小为 k 的（乘法）群。假设存在一个单射函数 φ:S→G 满足：若 x,y,z∈S 且 z=xy，则 φ(z)=φ(x)φ(y)。1990 年，Forcade 和 Pollington 猜想：若 S={1,2,…,k}，则 G 是阿贝尔群。这一直是一个长期猜想，被 Richard K. Guy 列为未解决问题，并且该问题似乎一直未解决，其未解决状态被 Caicedo 等人在 2021 年 [Electron. J. Combin.] 中指出。我们成功地在完全一般性下正面证明了 Forcade-Pollington 猜想。

    🌐 English Abstract

    Let $S$ denote a $k$-subset of $\mathbb{Z}$. Let $G$ be a (multiplicative) group of size $k$. Assume that there exists a function $\varphi\colon S \to G$ that is injective and that satisfies the following: If $x, y, z \in S$ and $z = xy$, then $\varphi(z) = \varphi(x) \varphi(y)$. In 1990, Forcade and Pollington conjectured that if $S = \{ 1, 2, \ldots, k \}$, then $G$ is abelian. This has been a long-standing conjecture highlighted by Richard K. Guy as an unsolved problem, and this problem appears to have remained open, with its open status having been noted by Caicedo et al. in 2021 [Electron. J. Combin.]. We succeed in proving the Forcade-Pollington conjecture in the affirmative and in full generality.

    💡 证明环簇与幂等半环簇的 Mal'cev 乘积等于其并，并刻画子直不可约成员和有限基。
    📖 中文摘要

    设 Rn 是满足 x^n≈x 的环簇，其中 n≥2，设 W 是加法交换的幂等半环簇。等式 Rn∘W=Rn∨W 成立，且该簇的子簇格为 L(Rn)×L(W)。其非加法幂等的子直不可约成员恰好有一个非平凡环分量，该分量是有限域。这样的成员由该域和一个带乘法单位的幂等半环确定；后者的单变量多项式函数的分离条件刻画了子直不可约性。每个子簇都有有限恒等基。一个四元半环生成一个子直不可约成员基数无界的簇。结果将 Wang 和 Shao 的 Mal'cev 乘积定理从吸收子簇推广到任意 W≤Slp。

    🌐 English Abstract

    Let $\Rn$ be the variety of rings satisfying $x^n\eqid x$, where $n\geq2$, and let $\W$ be a variety of idempotent semirings with commutative addition. The equality $\Rn\circ\W=\Rn\vee\W$ holds, and this variety has subvariety lattice $L(\Rn)\times L(\W)$. Its subdirectly irreducible members that are not additively idempotent have exactly one nontrivial ring component, which is a finite field. Such a member is determined by this field and an idempotent semiring with a multiplicative identity; a separation condition on unary polynomial functions of the latter characterizes subdirect irreducibility. Every subvariety has a finite identity basis. A four-element semiring generates a variety with subdirectly irreducible members of unbounded cardinality. The results extend the Mal'cev product theorem of Wang and Shao from the absorption subvariety to arbitrary $\W\leq\Slp$.

    🤖 AI 总结

    • 证明非平凡有限群单当且仅当无非平凡遗传 G-可置换子群。
    • 解决 Galt-Tyutyanov 猜想，回答 Skiba 问题。
    • 证明覆盖所有典型群，使用抛物子群下降和分解分类。
    💡 证明有限群单性当且仅当无非平凡遗传 G-可置换子群，解决 Galt-Tyutyanov 猜想。
    📖 中文摘要

    有限群 G 的子群 A 称为遗传 G-可置换的，如果对每个满足 A≤E≤G 的子群 E 和 E 的每个子群 B，B 的某个 G-共轭在 E 中与 A 可置换。我们给出有限群单性的新准则。一个非平凡有限群 G 是单群当且仅当它没有真非平凡遗传 G-可置换子群。这证明了 Galt 和 Tyutyanov 的猜想，并回答了 Kourovka 笔记本中问题 17.112 的 (b) 部分中 Skiba 的问题。早期工作解决了交错群、散在群、例外李型群以及群 PSL2(q) 和 PSU3(q) 的猜想。我们证明所有典型群的猜想。证明使用通过抛物子群的下降和有限单群分解的分类。

    🌐 English Abstract

    A subgroup $A$ of a finite group $G$ is \emph{hereditarily $G$-permutable} if, for every subgroup $E$ with $A\leq E\leq G$ and every subgroup $B$ of $E$, some $G$-conjugate of $B$ permutes with $A$ in $E$. We give a new criterion for a finite group to be simple. A nontrivial finite group $G$ is simple if and only if it has no proper nontrivial hereditarily $G$-permutable subgroup. This proves the conjecture of Galt and Tyutyanov and answers Skiba's question in part \textup{(b)} of Problem 17.112 in the Kourovka Notebook. Earlier work settled the conjecture for alternating groups, sporadic groups, exceptional groups of Lie type, and the groups ${\rm PSL}_2(q)$ and ${\rm PSU}_3(q)$. We prove the conjecture for all classical groups. The proof uses descent through parabolic subgroups and the classification of factorizations of finite simple groups.

    📖 中文摘要

    Whitehead 问题问是否每个满足 Ext1(A,Z)=0 的阿贝尔群 A 都是自由的，该问题独立于 ZFC。然而，这个问题在凝聚数学中有类似物，可以给出肯定回答。在本文中，我们给出一个新证明：大小为 κ 的阿贝尔群 A 是自由的当且仅当凝聚 Ext1(A,Z)(S_κ)=0，其中 S_κ 是 Add(ω,κ) 的布尔完备化的 Stone 空间。

    The Whitehead problem, which asks whether every abelian group $A$ satisfying $\mathrm{Ext}^1(A,\mathbb{Z}) = 0$ is free, is independent of ZFC. However, this problem has an analogue in condensed mathematics that can be answered affirmatively. In this note, we give a new proof that an abelian group $ A $ of size $\kappa$ is free if and only if $\underline{\mathrm{Ext}}^1(\underline{A}, \underline{\mathbb{Z}})(S_\kappa) = 0$, where $ S_\kappa $ is the Stone space of the Boolean completion of $\operatorname{Add}(\omega,\kappa)$.

We also show that a total order of the reflections yields an EL-labeling if it does so on all rank-two subintervals, which proves a conjecture of Williams for Coxeter elements. The quotient finite-edge graph is a connected bipartite ribbon graph in which even vertices have degree $1$ or $2$ and odd vertices may have any degree $d\mid q$; geometrically, degree $d$ records a $d$-cluster and an elliptic stabilizer of order $q/d$. We determine two finite ambiguities that arise when one passes from this quotient graph to a special polygon. First, all tree diagrams obtained from a fixed ribbon graph are classified by the possible spanning-tree cuts modulo graph automorphisms. Second, at a proper cluster one must record which cyclic gap contains the omitted branches of the universal $q$-star; every such choice develops to a special polygon. Combining the two gives an explicit finite description of all polygon orbits over a fixed dessin, together with a separate count of normalized realizations in the fixed tessellation. Concrete examples for $H_5$, $H_6$, and $H_7$ illustrate the geometry, and two arithmetic corollaries concern level-$2$ cusp labels and Galois orbits of principal congruence dessins.

    🌐 English Abstract

    📖 中文摘要

    🌐 English Abstract

This answers a cohomological divisibility question posed by Natale. Combining the group-theoretic divisibility with Natale's exponent bound and a lifting argument, we prove Kashina's exponent conjecture, in the arbitrary-field formulation of Etingof and Gelaki, for every finite-dimensional semisimple and cosemisimple Hopf algebra $H$ over a field $k$ for which $\operatorname{Rep}(H\otimes_k\overline{k})$ is group-theoretical. The same argument proves the corresponding degree-three cohomological divisibility for coefficients in an arbitrary $G$-module. For complex group-theoretical categories, we also establish Frobenius-Schur exponent divisibility under a cohomological factorization hypothesis, without assuming a fiber functor.     🌐 English Abstract

    This paper investigates the theory of fractional Sobolev mappings between Carnot groups, with a particular focus on the Korevaar-Schoen energy functional and interpolation properties. We establish that weak contact equations hold for fractional Sobolev mappings on Carnot groups, generalizing classical results from the Euclidean setting to the sub-Riemannian framework. Our main results include: (1) the proof that fractional Sobolev mappings satisfying weak contact equations belong to the horizontal fractional Sobolev space $W_H^{s-\lambda,q}(\Omega;G_2)$ for any sufficiently small $\lambda>0$; (2) interpolation theorems for fractional Sobolev mappings between Carnot groups, demonstrating that the limiting behavior as $s \to 1$ recovers the first-order Sobolev space; (3) a rigorous treatment of the Korevaar-Schoen energy on Carnot groups, including a characterization of its minimizers via horizontal differential forms.

  3. [2610.11596] 关于度量空间距离中心对应的图
    On the Graph Corresponding to Center of Distances of Metric Space
    👥 Oleksiy Dovgoshey, Olga Rovenska
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 研究度量空间的距离中心对应的中心图。
    • 证明有限度量空间中心图边数下界为ceil(|Y|/2)，超度量空间为|X|-1。
    • 刻画达到等号的中心图。
    • 为度量空间与图论的交叉提供新结果。
    💡 研究度量空间中心图，证明有限度量空间边数下界，并刻画达到等号的中心图。
    📖 中文摘要

    度量空间(Y,ρ)的距离中心C(Y)是所有t≥0的集合，使得对于每个点p∈Y，方程ρ(y,p)=t有解。我们说简单图CG_Y是度量空间(Y,ρ)的中心图，如果Y是CG_Y的顶点集，且不同顶点x,y∈Y相邻当且仅当ρ(x,y)∈C(Y)。我们证明对于所有有限度量空间(Y,ρ)，|Y|≥2且CG_Y非空，有|E(CG_Y)|≥ceil(1/2|Y|)。还证明对于每个有限超度量空间(X,d)，不等式|E(CG_X)|≥|X|-1成立。满足|E(CG_Y)|=ceil(1/2|Y|)和|E(CG_X)|=|X|-1的度量空间和超度量空间的中心图被描述到图同构。

    🌐 English Abstract

    The center of distances $C(Y)$ of a metric space $(Y,\rho)$ is the set of all $t\geq 0$ for which the equation $\rho(y,p)=t $ has a solution for each point $p\in Y$. We say that a simple graph $CG_Y$ is the central graph of a metric space $(Y,\rho)$ if $Y$ is the vertex set of $CG_Y$ and distinct vertices $x,y\in Y$ are adjacent if and only if $\rho(x,y)\in C(Y)$. We prove the inequality $|E(CG_Y)| \geq \left\lceil \frac{1}{2}|Y| \right\rceil $ for all finite metric spaces $(Y,\rho)$ with $|Y|\geq 2$ and non-empty $CG_Y.$ It is also proved that the inequality $|E(CG_X)| \geq |X|-1 $ holds for each finite ultrametric space $(X,d)$. The central graphs of metric spaces $(Y,\rho)$ and ultrametric spaces $(X,d)$ satisfying $|E(CG_Y)|=\left\lceil \frac{1}{2}|Y| \right\rceil $ and, respectively, $|E(CG_X)|=|X|-1 $ are described up to graph isomorphism.

    👉 https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11596
  4. [2610.10665] 整数格中的 Erdős-Falconer 距离问题
    The Erd\H{o}s-Falconer distance problem in the integer lattice
    👥 Eyvindur Ari Palsson, Jian-An Wang
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 提出整数格中的 Erdős-Falconer 距离问题。
    • 动机来自 Magyar 离散模拟和 Falconer 距离问题。
    • 使用离散球面平均的 ℓ^p-改进不等式，首次应用此类界。
    • 在某些区域方法优于 Erdős 不同距离问题的最强界。
    💡 提出并研究整数格中的 Erdős-Falconer 距离问题，使用离散球面平均的 ℓ^p-改进不等式。
    📖 中文摘要

    在本文中，我们提出并研究所谓的整数格中的 Erdős-Falconer 距离问题。一个动机是 Magyar 对 Furstenberg、Katznelson 和 Weiss 结果的离散模拟，他们证明在 R^d（d≥2）中正上 Banach 密度的集合必须出现所有任意大的距离。另一个动机是 Falconer 距离问题，它可以视为 Steinhaus 结果的精化，将假设从正 Lebesgue 测度降低到仅询问集合的维数。在此背景下，我们因此希望降低正上 Banach 密度的假设，同时获得保证大量不同距离的陈述。所有这些问题也与 Erdős 不同距离问题相关。我们的命名如同该问题的有限域变体，承认问题的一部分与 Erdős 不同距离问题相似，而另一部分与 Falconer 距离问题相似。我们使用的主要工具是离散球面平均的 ℓ^p-改进不等式，据我们所知这是此类界的首次应用。可以使用 Erdős 不同距离问题的结果来推进我们的猜想，但令人惊讶的是，即使使用 Solymosi 和 Vu 在高维中的最强界，在某些区域我们的方法更好。虽然解决 Erdős 不同距离问题将蕴含我们的无约束猜想，但 Erdős 或 Falconer 距离问题的任何结果似乎都不蕴含我们的有约束变体的任何结果。我们将此问题视为一个模型问题，最终可能反过来贡献于原始的有约束距离问题。

    🌐 English Abstract

    In this paper we propose and study what we call the Erd\H{o}s-Falconer distance problem in the integer lattice. One motivation is Magyar's discrete analog of the result of Furstenberg, Katznelson, and Weiss, who showed that all arbitrarily large distances had to appear for a set of positive upper Banach density in $\mathbb{R}^d$, $d\geq 2$. Another motivation is the Falconer distance problem, which can be viewed as a refinement of a result of Steinhaus, dropping assumptions from positive Lebesgue measure to merely asking about the dimension of sets. In this context we thus want to lower the assumption of positive upper Banach density and yet obtain statements that guarantee that we have lots of distinct distances. All of these questions are also related to the Erd\H{o}s distinct distance problem. Our naming is as for the finite field variant of the problem, acknowledging that parts of the problem share similarities with the Erd\H{o}s distinct distance problem while others share similarities with the Falconer distance problem. The main tool we use are $\ell^p$-improving inequalities for discrete spherical averages, which to the best of our knowledge is the first application of such bounds. One can use results on the Erd\H{o}s distinct distance problem to make progress on our conjecture but surprisingly, even if using the strongest available bounds in high dimensions using the mechanism of Solymosi and Vu, then in certain regimes our methods do better. While a resolution of the Erd\H{o}s distinct distance problem would imply our unpinned conjecture then no results on either the Erd\H{o}s or Falconer distance problem seem to imply any results for our pinned variant. We view this problem as a model problem that might ultimately contribute back to the original pinned distance problems.

    👉 https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10665

    📖 中文摘要

    我们引入凸体协变差函数的光滑径向近似。该构造用 K 与 K+x 的径向 (-p)-平均代替交集 K∩(K+x)，得到一族 g_{K,p}，当 p→∞ 时逐点收敛于经典协变差函数。对每个固定 p>0，函数 g_{K,p} 在原点附近光滑，其 Hessian 可由定义极 L2 投影体的算子显式表示。我们研究 p→∞ 与水平亏损 δ=p^α（α<0）的同时极限。归一化水平集呈现三种区域：当 α>-1 时出现极投影体，当 α<-1 时出现极 L2 投影体，而在 α=-1 时出现临界过渡体。该过渡体由显式的 log cosh 型边界积分描述，将临界区域自然地置于 Orlicz 投影体框架内。

    🌐 English Abstract

    We introduce a smooth radial approximation of the covariogram of a convex body. For each fixed \(p>0\), the function \(g_{K,p}\) is smooth near the origin, and its Hessian is computed explicitly in terms of the operator defining the polar \(L_2\)-projection body. We study the simultaneous limit in which \(p\to\infty\) and the level deficit is \(\delta=p^\alpha\), \(\alpha<0\). The normalized level sets exhibit three regimes: the polar projection body appears for \(\alpha>-1\), the polar \(L_2\)-projection body appears for \(\alpha<-1\), and a critical transition body appears at \(\alpha=-1\). This transition body is described by an explicit \(\log\cosh\)-type boundary integral, placing the critical regime naturally within the framework of Orlicz projection bodies.

    将 Wasserstein 空间的子集嵌入线性空间是一个兼具理论与计算意义的问题。本文证明，Wasserstein 空间到非平凡型 Banach 空间的三种经典自然嵌入在由矩界给出的子集上是双 Hölder 的。这三种嵌入分别是线性化最优传输嵌入、切片 Wasserstein 嵌入和一种特定的核均值嵌入，均在多个应用中被考虑。我们的结果与已知的将整个 Wasserstein 空间嵌入非平凡型 Banach 空间的障碍形成对比。我们还证明了关于所需矩条件的结果的尖锐性。
