    Embedding subsets of Wasserstein spaces into linear spaces is a problem of both theoretical and computational interest. In this work, we prove that three classical and natural embeddings of Wasserstein spaces into Banach spaces of non-trivial type are bi-H\"older on subsets of Wasserstein spaces given by moment bounds. These embeddings, all considered in multiple applications, are the linearized optimal transport embedding, the sliced-Wasserstein embedding, and a particular kernel mean embedding. Our results contrast with known obstructions to embeddin the full Wasserstein space into Banach spaces of non-trivial type. We also show the sharpness of our results regarding the moments required for our conclusions to hold.

    👉 https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12029
  12. [2610.04834] Lee 度量下的量子码
    Quantum codes in the Lee metric
    👥 Jinkang Guo, Yiqiu Han, Aranya Chakraborty, Shubham P. Jain, Tianhao Liu, Victor V. Albert, Andrew Lucas
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 引入 Lee 度量下的量子编码框架以处理小位移噪声。
    • 对 Z_q 稳定子码发展联合与分离 Lee 度量，构造高效 qubit 码。
    • 证明 CSS Lee-LDPC 码的 Lee 距离上界为 O(n)。
    • 发现螺旋重复码在有限温度下具有指数长记忆时间。
    💡 建立 Lee 度量量子编码框架，构造稳定子码并证明 Lee 距离上界与自校正性质。
    📖 中文摘要

    我们为离散小位移噪声引入量子编码框架，其中 qudit 上的错误被建模为低权 X 型和 Z 型 Pauli 位移，类似于连续变量系统中的小相空间位移。该结构近似被核自旋噪声所尊重，并由 Lee 度量刻画，从而推动经典 Lee 度量编码理论的量子推广。我们对任意 q 的 Z_q 上的稳定子码发展了这一形式，对错误的 X 和 Z 分量使用联合与分离 Lee 度量。对于 qubit，联合度量将 Y 错误计数两次，可产生比传统 Hamming 度量码用更少物理 qubit 检测和纠正 X 与 Z 错误的码。我们讨论 Clifford 门下的 Lee 权扩散，并通过 Gray 映射将 Z_4 上的码 qubit 化，发现具有两重横向非 qubit-Clifford 门的码。对于 n 个 qudit 上的量子 CSS Lee-LDPC 码，我们证明 Lee 距离不能超过 O(n)，且对 q 一致，这表明使用大 q qudit 的大内部 Hilbert 空间来构造高 Lee 距离码存在意想不到的障碍。在局部 Metropolis 动力学下，某些经典 q 元螺旋重复码在固定温度下具有指数长的记忆时间（q 足够大且随系统长度增长），这可理解为有限温度下的自发对称破缺，即使在局部一维模型中也是如此。这些螺旋重复码的超图乘积提供局部二维量子码，继承了对 Z 错误的自校正，但对 X 错误的自校正仍是开放问题。

    🌐 English Abstract

    We introduce a quantum coding framework for discrete small-shift noise, in which errors on qudits are modeled as low-weight $X$- and $Z$-type Pauli shifts, analogous to small phase-space displacements in continuous-variable systems. This structure is approximately respected by nuclear-spin noise and captured by the Lee metric, motivating a quantum extension of classical Lee-metric coding theory. We develop such a formalism for stabilizer codes over $\mathbb{Z}_q$ for arbitrary $q$, using joint and separate Lee metrics for the $X$- and $Z$-components of errors. For qubits, the joint metric counts $Y$ errors twice and can yield codes that detect and correct $X$ and $Z$ errors with fewer physical qubits than codes designed for the conventional Hamming metric. We discuss Lee-weight spreading under Clifford gates and qubitize codes over $\mathbb{Z}_4$ via the Gray map, finding codes with two-fold transversal non-qubit-Clifford gates. For quantum CSS Lee-LDPC codes on $n$ qudits, we prove that the Lee distance cannot exceed $O(n)$, uniformly in $q$, demonstrating an unexpected obstruction to using the large internal Hilbert space of a large-$q$ qudit to make high-Lee-distance codes. Under local Metropolis dynamics, certain classical $q$-ary ``helical repetition codes'' have exponentially long memory times at fixed temperature for sufficiently large $q$ (that grows with system length), which can be understood as spontaneous symmetry breaking at finite temperature, even in local one-dimensional models. Hypergraph products of these helical repetition codes provide local two-dimensional quantum codes that inherit self-correction for $Z$ errors, but self-correction for $X$ errors remains an open question.

    👉 https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04834
  13. [2610.11814] Gauss-Chebyshev 求积、S1-设计及平面 Minkowski 逆问题：设计诱导凸几何的一维模型
    Gauss--Chebyshev Quadrature, $\mathbb S^1$-Designs, and the Planar Minkowski Inverse Problem: A One-Dimensional Model for Design-Induced Convex Geometry
    👥 Congpei An
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 研究等权圆求积与平面 Minkowski 重构的关系。
    • 给出中心 Minkowski 多边形到圆盘的精确 Hausdorff 距离。
    • 建立 W1 距离与 Hausdorff 距离的 π 倍不等式。
    • 利用稀疏偏差谱得到 O(N^{-2}) 的尖锐速率。
    💡 研究等权圆求积与平面 Minkowski 重构的关系，给出 Hausdorff 距离精确公式与 W1 界。
    📖 中文摘要

    我们研究等权圆求积、Gauss-Chebyshev 求积与平面 Minkowski 重构之间的关系。规则 N 点网格是强度 N-1 的 S1-设计；2M 点网格的半步步旋转投影到经典 M 点 Gauss-Chebyshev 规则。一族共同的求积误差泛函给出积分误差、混合面积误差和支撑函数误差的精确恒等式。对于 N≥3，周长为 2π 的中心 Minkowski 多边形 P_N 到单位圆盘 B2 的 Hausdorff 距离精确为 d_H(P_N,B2)=1-(π/N)cot(π/N)=π^2/(3N^2)+O(N^{-4})。使用 Steiner 中心化，我们得到对平衡非退化概率测度 d_H(K_μ,K_ν)≤π W1(μ,ν)，其中 W1 使用测地距离。其指数即使在均匀测度附近的平滑正密度下也是尖锐的。恒等式 W1(ν_N,σ)=π/(2N) 给出规则网格的一般 O(N^{-1}) 界。更尖锐的 O(N^{-2}) 速率来自稀疏偏差谱和逆乘子 (1-k^2)^{-1}。最后，一个扰动估计给出保持二次速率及其主导常数的充分条件。

    🌐 English Abstract

    We study the relation between equal-weight circle quadrature, Gauss--Chebyshev quadrature, and planar Minkowski reconstruction. The regular $N$-point grid is an $\mathbb S^1$-design of strength $N-1$; a half-step rotation of the $2M$-point grid projects to the classical $M$-point Gauss--Chebyshev rule. A common family of quadrature-error functionals gives exact identities for integration error, mixed-area error, and support-function error. For $N\ge3$, the centered Minkowski polygon $P_N$ with perimeter $2\pi$ has the exact Hausdorff distance from the unit disk $B^2$ \[ d_H(P_N,B^2)=1-\frac{\pi}{N}\cot\frac{\pi}{N} =\frac{\pi^2}{3N^2}+O(N^{-4}). \] With Steiner centering, we obtain $d_H(K_\mu,K_\nu)\le\pi W_1(\mu,\nu)$ for balanced, nondegenerate probability measures, where $W_1$ uses geodesic distance. Its exponent is sharp even for smooth positive densities near the uniform measure. The identity $W_1(\nu_N,\sigma)=\pi/(2N)$ yields a general $O(N^{-1})$ bound for the regular grid. The sharper $O(N^{-2})$ rate follows from the sparse discrepancy spectrum and the inverse multiplier $(1-k^2)^{-1}$. Finally, a perturbation estimate gives sufficient conditions for preserving the quadratic rate and its leading constant.

    👉 https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11814
  14. [2610.12301] 不可测染色的可测障碍
    Measurable obstructions for unmeasurable colourings
    👥 James Davies
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 研究欧氏空间可测与普通染色数是否相等的问题。
    • 证明 d≥4 时两者相等，并推广到有理点集与多距离情形。
    • 得到多距离染色数的指数下界。
    • 改进小维数染色数的下界，并展望推广到 d=2,3。
    💡 证明高维欧氏空间的可测与普通染色数相等，并改进多个距离的染色数下界。
    📖 中文摘要

    由于强大分析技术的可用性，欧氏空间的可测染色数已知下界远优于普通染色数。事实上，即使著名的 Hadwiger-Nelson 问题的突破性下界 5 也比可测情形的下界晚了 35 年以上。这引发了 Székely 在 1984 年猜想的基本问题：欧氏空间的可测与普通染色数是否不同。我们的主要结果是 α̅(R4)=m1(R4) 和 χ(R4)=χ(m)(R4)，且对 d≥5 有 α̅(Qd)=α̅(Rd)=m1(Rd) 和 χ(Qd)=χ(Rd)=χ(m)(Rd)。我们的定理对多个禁止距离 D={d1,...,dt} 也成立，只要 d1^2,...,dt^2∈Q。作为推论，我们立即将大量可测染色数结果提升到普通情形。我们还进一步优化了新界。对于多个距离，Erdős 问当禁止距离数 k 增长时，Rd 的染色数是否指数增长。通过 Bukh 的定理，我们对 d≥4 得到 sup_{|D|=k} χ_D(Rd) ≥ m1(Rd)^{-k}。在 Erdős 的另一问题上取得进展，我们证明 (2+o(1))^d ≤ χ(Rd) ≤ (3√3/4+o(1))^d。我们还大幅改进了小 d≥4 时 χ(Rd) 的下界。我们期望我们的技术可以进一步发展，包括将主要定理 χ(Rd)=χ(m)(Rd) 对 d≥4 推广到 d=3 甚至 d=2 以处理 Hadwiger-Nelson 问题。

    🌐 English Abstract

    Due to the availability of powerful analytic techniques, vastly superior lower bounds are known for the measurable chromatic number of Euclidean spaces compared to their ordinary chromatic number. Indeed, even the breakthrough lower bound of 5 for the famous Hadwiger-Nelson problem lagged over 35 years behind that of the measurable setting. This raises the fundamental question of whether the measurable and ordinary chromatic number of Euclidean spaces differ as conjectured by Sz\'ekely in 1984. Our main result is that $\overline\alpha(\mathbb{R}^4)=m_1(\mathbb{R}^4)$ and $\chi(\mathbb{R}^4)=\chi^{(m)}(\mathbb{R}^4)$, and for $d\ge5$ that \[ \overline\alpha(\mathbb{Q}^d) = \overline\alpha(\mathbb{R}^d)=m_1(\mathbb{R}^d) \qquad\text{and}\qquad \chi(\mathbb{Q}^d) = \chi(\mathbb{R}^d)=\chi^{(m)}(\mathbb{R}^d). \] Our theorem also holds for multiple forbidden distances $D=\{d_1,\ldots,d_t\}$ provided that $d_1^2,\ldots,d_t^2 \in \mathbb{Q}$. As a consequence, we immediately lift numerous measurable chromatic number results into the ordinary setting. We also take the opportunity to further optimize the new bounds. For multiple distances, Erd\H{o}s asked whether the chromatic number of $\mathbb{R}^d$ with up to $k$ forbidden distances $D$ grows exponentially in $k$. By a theorem of Bukh, we obtain for $d \ge 4$ that \[ \sup_{|D|=k}\chi_D(\mathbb{R}^d) \ge m_1(\mathbb{R}^d)^{-k}. \] Making progress on another problem of Erd\H{o}s, we prove that \[ (2+o(1))^d \le \chi(\mathbb{R}^d) \le \left(\frac{3\sqrt{3}}{4}+o(1)\right)^d. \] We also vastly improve the lower bounds for $\chi(\mathbb{R}^d)$ for small $d\ge4$. We expect that our techniques could be developed much further. This includes the possibility of extending our main theorem that $\chi(\mathbb{R}^d)=\chi^{(m)}(\mathbb{R}^d)$ for $d\ge 4$ to $d=3$ or possibly even to $d=2$ to tackle the Hadwiger-Nelson problem.

    Directed fractal percolation and non-Lipschitz variants
    👥 Shirshendu Ganguly, Victor Ginsburg, Kaihao Jing
    🤖 AI 总结

    • 发展多尺度框架证明通道时间与线性增长的多项式差距。
    • 障碍在于有向路径的 Lipschitz 性质。
    • 引入不可整流有向渗流，构造指数快速移动的有向路径。
    💡 研究分形渗流中的有向渗流，证明通道时间与线性增长的多项式差距，并引入不可整流有向渗流。
    📖 中文摘要

    虽然具有快速衰减尾部的随机独立同分布无序中的无向和有向几何模型预期表现相似并展现 Kardar-Parisi-Zhang 普适类特征，但当背景噪声是分形时，情形预期显著不同。后者的典型例子是 Liouville 量子引力，其中平面高斯自由场形成分形背景。一个捕捉部分本质特征的玩具模型是 Mandelbrot 的分形渗流，即随机 Cantor 集。在最简单设置中，不同尺度的二进立方体以概率 p 独立保留，所有保留立方体的交集形成开点集。此类集合的连通性已被深入研究。在 [CCD88, Cha95] 中证明了一个令人惊讶的结果：虽然分形渗流集的连通性存在相变（如通常的键渗流），但有向渗流从不发生。几何测度论中也获得了类似精神的结果。作为理解由高斯自由场驱动的最后通道渗流（LPP）的一步（由 [GGN24] 发起），我们研究分形渗流中 LPP 的量化问题。通过发展多尺度框架，我们证明通道时间与线性增长之间存在几乎多项式的差距。高 LPP 值的障碍是有向路径的 Lipschitz 性质。沿着这些思路，[Cha96] 证明分形渗流的任何连通子集的 Hausdorff 维数必须严格大于一。我们证明可以构造通过开点的有向路径，只要它们在空间方向上指数快速移动——我们引入并称之为不可整流有向渗流的模型。

    🌐 English Abstract

    While models of undirected and directed geometry in random i.i.d. disorder with rapidly decaying tails are expected to behave similarly and exhibit features of the Kardar--Parisi--Zhang universality class, the scenario when the background noise is fractal is expected to be significantly different. A canonical example of the latter is Liouville Quantum Gravity, where the planar Gaussian free field forms the fractal background. A toy model capturing some of the essential features is given by Mandelbrot's fractal percolation, a random Cantor set. In the simplest setting, dyadic cubes of different scales are retained independently with probability $p$ (a parameter of the model), and the intersection of all retained cubes forms the set of open sites. Connectivity properties of such sets have been intensely studied. Across [CCD88, Cha95], a surprising result was proven. Namely, while there is a phase transition for connectivity of the fractal percolation set (like in usual bond percolation), directed percolation never occurs. Results of a similar spirit have been obtained in geometric measure theory as well. As a step towards understanding last passage percolation (LPP) driven by the Gaussian free field (initiated in [GGN24]), we study the problem of quantifying LPP in fractal percolation. Developing a multi-scale framework, we show an almost polynomial gap from linear growth of the passage time. The obstruction to high LPP values is the Lipschitz nature of directed paths. Along these lines, [Cha96] showed that any connected subset of fractal percolation must have Hausdorff dimension strictly larger than one. We show that one can construct directed paths passing through open sites in fractal percolation provided they move exponentially fast in the spatial direction---a model we introduce and term as unrectifiable directed percolation.

  1. [2610.11390] 数域的弱与真正 A_n-形式性
    Weak and genuine A_n-formality for number fields
    👥 Ambrus Pál, Gereon Quick
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 研究数域 Galois 上链代数的 A_n-形式性。
    • 引入弱 A_n-形式性，通过有限嵌入问题刻画。
    • 证明每个有限 A_3-嵌入问题可解，但整体不真正 A_3-形式。

    📖 中文摘要

    我们为 Galois 上链代数引入弱 A_n-形式性，并对 mod-p 上同调集中在至多二次的投射有限群，通过有限嵌入问题及其形成的投射系统刻画弱和真正 A_n-形式性。然后我们研究数域的 A_n-形式性。设 K 是数域，p 是奇素数且 μ_p⊄K。对于 p≥5，以及 p=3 在自然局部分圆条件下，我们证明每个关联的有限 A_3-嵌入问题可解。相反，对于每个奇素数 p 且 μ_p⊄K，我们证明不存在相容的有限阶段解族，因此 C•(G_K,F_p) 不是真正 A_3-形式的。证明使用 Chebotarev 密度、控制域和 Gras-Munnier 准则来阻碍有限阶段解的相容性，并建立在 Maire-Minač-Ramakrishna-Tân 先前工作的基础上。这表明真正 A_3-形式性检测到数域的全局相容性阻碍，该阻碍对每个单独有限嵌入问题不可见，对来自单个强 Massey 消失问题的阻碍也不可见。

    🌐 English Abstract

    We introduce weak $A_n$-formality for Galois cochain algebras and, for profinite groups whose mod-$p$ cohomology is concentrated in degrees at most two, characterise weak and genuine $A_n$-formality through finite embedding problems and the projective system they form. We then study $A_n$-formality for number fields. Let $K$ be a number field and let $p$ be an odd prime with $\mu_p\not\subset K$. For $p\geq 5$, and for $p=3$ under a natural local cyclotomic condition, we prove that every associated finite $A_3$-embedding problem is solvable. In contrast, for every odd $p$ with $\mu_p\not\subset K$, we show that no compatible family of finite-stage solutions exists, and hence $C^\bullet(G_K,\mathbb F_p)$ is not genuinely $A_3$-formal. The proof uses Chebotarev density, governing fields, and the Gras-Munnier criterion to obstruct the compatibility of finite-stage solutions, and it builds on previous work of Maire-Min\'a\v{c}-Ramakrishna-T\^an. This shows that genuine $A_3$-formality detects a global compatibility obstruction for number fields invisible to every individual finite embedding problem and invisible to obstructions arising from individual strong Massey vanishing problems.

  2. [2610.10572] 和式 sum_{i=1}^n 1/gcd(n,i) 的整数值
    The Integer Values of $\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{1}{\gcd(n,i)}$

    We show that an ir{\-}reducible cuspidal eta quotient of level~$N$ is uniquely identified by its orders of vanishing at $O((\log_2 N)^{r-1})$ cusps, where $r$ denotes the number of distinct prime divisors of $N$. In particular, all such eta quotients which are Fricke eigenforms of a prime power level, are uniquely identified only by their orders of vanishing at~$\infty$. We also prove that for all $k\in\N$, there exists a primitive and irreducible cuspidal eta quotient of weight $k/2$.     👥 K. Srinivasa Raghava
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 研究 Rogers-Ramanujan 连分数的普通与对数 Mellin 矩。
    • 证明倒数尖点系数为二次无理代数整数，对数系数为二次无理数。
    • 确定三个矩生成函数的精确极点。
    • 建立反射恒等式与截断估计。
    💡 研究 Rogers-Ramanujan 连分数的对数 Mellin 矩，证明系数为二次无理数并确定极点。
    📖 中文摘要

    我们研究 Rogers-Ramanujan 连分数的两个尖点展开如何控制其普通与对数 Mellin 矩。Ramanujan 的因子分解将对数变换表示为 zeta 与二次 Dirichlet L 函数乘积的组合。其相邻整数与半整数值给出相应奇数特殊值的可逆坐标变换。我们证明每个倒数尖点系数是二次无理代数整数，具有显式最小多项式，且每个对数系数是二次无理数。这些事实确定了三个归一化矩生成函数的精确极点。对数矩生成函数具有绝对收敛的部分分式展开，在每个正平方数处有极点。对于普通矩，双尖点不完全 gamma 展开给出在每个正分裂点处的亚纯延拓。一个一般 Mellin 引理给出相应的非全纯性陈述。我们还建立反射恒等式、垂直带衰减和显式截断估计。

    🌐 English Abstract

    We study how the two cusp expansions of the Rogers-Ramanujan continued fraction control its ordinary and logarithmic Mellin moments. Ramanujan's factorizations express the logarithmic transform as a combination of products of zeta and quadratic Dirichlet $L$-functions. Its adjacent integer and half-integer values give an invertible change of coordinates for the corresponding odd special values. We prove that every reciprocal-cusp coefficient is a quadratic irrational algebraic integer, with an explicit minimal polynomial, and that every logarithmic coefficient is quadratic irrational. These facts determine the exact poles of three normalized-moment generating functions. The logarithmic one has an absolutely convergent partial fraction expansion with a pole at every positive square. For the ordinary moments, a two-cusp incomplete-gamma expansion gives meromorphic continuation at every positive splitting point. A general Mellin lemma yields the corresponding nonholonomicity statements. We also establish reflection identities, vertical strip decay and an explicit truncation estimate.

  5. [2610.10582] 素数间隙中粗糙数的重数常数
    Multiplicity Constants for Rough Numbers in Prime Gaps
    👥 Bahar Uğurdoğan
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 研究素数间隙中粗糙数个数小于 r 的计数 N_r(X)。
    • 定义常数 c_r 并证明其渐近公式与精确估计。
    • 结合 Montgomery-Vaughan 矩界与端点条件移除。
    • 给出 c 的数值下界 c≥2.7413。
    💡 研究素数间隙中粗糙数个数小于 r 的计数，定义常数 c_r 并给出渐近公式。
    📖 中文摘要

    设 p_n 为第 n 个素数，g_n=p_{n+1}-p_n。遵循 Gafni 和 Tao，若整数 m 满足 p_n<m<p_{n+1} 且其最小素因子至少为 g_n，则称 m 对该间隙是粗糙的；设 M_n 为这样的整数个数。Gafni 和 Tao 证明满足 X≤p_n≤2X 且 M_n=0 的 n 的个数为 O(X/log^2 X)，并在某种 Dickson-Hardy-Littlewood 素数元组猜想下渐近于 cX/log^2 X。我们研究 N_r(X)，即满足 X≤p_n≤2X 且 M_n<r 的 n 的个数。对每个 r≥1，我们通过端点条件局部筛模型定义常数 c_r，其中 c_1=c，并证明无条件地 c_r=h_r^*+O_ε(r^{1/2}(log r)^{1+ε}) 对 r≥3，其中 h_r^* 是最小偶数 h≥2 满足 h∏_{p<h}(1-1/p)≥r。因此 c_r=H_r(1+O(e^{-κ√log r})) 对绝对常数 κ>0，其中 H_r>e 解 e^{-γ}H_r/log H_r=r（γ 为 Euler 常数），且 c_r=e^γ r(log r+log log r+γ+(log log r+γ)/log r+O((log log r)^2/(log r)^2))。证明结合短区间中既约剩余的 Montgomery-Vaughan 矩界与端点条件的精确移除。对每个固定 r，Gafni 和 Tao 的论证可推广给出无条件 N_r(X)≪_r X/log^2 X，并在其猜想下 N_r(X)∼c_r X/log^2 X。局部模型的精确枚举给出 c≥2.7413。

    🌐 English Abstract

    Let $p_n$ be the $n$th prime and $g_n=p_{n+1}-p_n$. Following Gafni and Tao, an integer $m$ with $p_n<m<p_{n+1}$ is rough for the gap if its least prime factor is at least $g_n$; let $M_n$ be the number of such integers. Gafni and Tao proved that the number of $n$ with $X\le p_n\le 2X$ and $M_n=0$ is $O(X/\log^2 X)$, and that it is asymptotic to $cX/\log^2 X$ under a form of the Dickson-Hardy-Littlewood prime tuples conjecture. We study $N_r(X)$, the number of $n$ with $X\le p_n\le 2X$ and $M_n<r$. For every $r\ge 1$ we define a constant $c_r$ by an endpoint-conditioned local sieve model, with $c_1=c$, and we prove unconditionally that $c_r=h_r^*+O_\varepsilon(r^{1/2}(\log r)^{1+\varepsilon})$ for $r\ge 3$, where $h_r^*$ is the least even $h\ge 2$ with $h\prod_{p<h}(1-1/p)\ge r$. Consequently $c_r=H_r(1+O(e^{-\kappa\sqrt{\log r}}))$ for an absolute constant $\kappa>0$, where $H_r>e$ solves $e^{-\gamma}H_r/\log H_r=r$ ($\gamma$ is Euler's constant), and $c_r=e^\gamma r(\log r+\log\log r+\gamma+(\log\log r+\gamma)/\log r+O((\log\log r)^2/(\log r)^2))$. The proof combines the Montgomery-Vaughan moment bounds for reduced residues in short intervals with an exact removal of the endpoint conditioning. For each fixed $r$, the argument of Gafni and Tao extends to give $N_r(X)\ll_r X/\log^2 X$ unconditionally, and $N_r(X)\sim c_rX/\log^2 X$ under their conjecture. Exact enumeration of the local model gives $c\ge 2.7413$.

    👉 https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10582

    Finite-radius Jensen and relative-entropy formulations of the Balazard--Saias--Yor criterion
    👥 Henning Wunderlich
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 给出 Riemann 假设的 Balazard-Saias-Yor 准则的两种显式重构。
    • 利用 Jensen 公式分离平凡零点的发散贡献。
    • 通过相对熵饱和等价刻画 Riemann 假设。
    • 强调这是有限半径处理与信息论重构，非新证明。
    💡 给出 Riemann 假设的 Balazard-Saias-Yor 型准则的两种显式重构：有限半径 Jensen 公式与相对熵饱和。
    📖 中文摘要

    我们给出 Riemann 假设的 Balazard-Saias-Yor 型准则的两种显式重构。首先，将 Jensen 公式应用于 u(s)=(s-1)ζ(s)，在将单位圆盘映射到 Re s<1/2 后。对于中心对应 s=-1/2 的归一化，平凡零点的像 z_n=(4n-1)/(4n+3) 给出一个伸缩 Jensen 乘积。这在有限半径下分离出平凡零点的完全发散贡献；剩余缺陷是临界线外非平凡零点映射的非负积分计数。单参数版本给出 gamma 商乘积和显式有限部分。其次，在通过已建立的边界恒等式后，我们组合两个 Poisson 权得到 Riemann Ξ 函数的绝对收敛对数积分。从随机变量 Z=|Ξ(T)/Ξ(0)|^2 定义其分布 μ 和尺寸偏置分布 ν(dz)=zμ(dz)/M。数据处理不等式对该统计量无损，Riemann 假设等价于 D_KL(μ||ν) 的显式上界的饱和。我们还记录相关的 Donsker-Varadhan 和矩形式，并通过 Riemann 正 Fourier 核表示目标常数。这里使用的解析边界准则和 Jensen-Hardy 机制是已知的。本文目的是对平凡零点的显式有限半径处理和信息论重构，而非 Riemann 假设的新证明或独立边界积分准则。

    🌐 English Abstract

    We give two explicit reformulations of a Balazard--Saias--Yor type criterion for the Riemann hypothesis. First, Jensen's formula is applied to $u(s)=(s-1)\zeta(s)$ after mapping the unit disk onto $Re s<1/2$. For the normalization whose center corresponds to $s=-1/2$, the images $z_n=(4n-1)/(4n+3)$ of the trivial zeros yield a telescoping Jensen product. This isolates, at finite radius, the complete divergent contribution of the trivial zeros; the remaining defect is a nonnegative integrated count of mapped nontrivial zeros off the critical line. A one-parameter version gives a gamma-quotient product and an explicit finite part. Second, after passing through the established boundary identity, we combine two Poisson weights to obtain an absolutely convergent logarithmic integral of the Riemann $\Xi$-function. From the random variable $Z=|\Xi(T)/\Xi(0)|^2$ we define its law $\mu$ and the size-biased law $\nu(\,\mathrm d z)=z\mu(\,\mathrm d z)/M$. The data-processing inequality is lossless for this statistic, and the Riemann hypothesis is equivalent to saturation of an explicit upper bound for $D_{\rm KL}(\mu\|\nu)$. We also record the associated Donsker--Varadhan and moment forms and express the target constant through Riemann's positive Fourier kernel. The analytic boundary criterion and Jensen--Hardy mechanism used here are known. The purpose of the paper is an explicit finite-radius treatment of the trivial zeros and an information-theoretic reformulation, not a new proof of the Riemann hypothesis or an independent boundary-integral criterion.

    👉 https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10584

  10. [2610.10660] 三个二次 Collatz 型映射的轨道分类
    Classification of the Orbits of Three Quadratic Collatz-type Maps
    👥 Xuda Ye
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 研究三个二次 Collatz 型映射的轨道分类。
    • 将问题化为指数 Diophantine 方程 2^a±2^b+1=z^2。
    • 利用 Szalay 的分类确定所有轨道行为。
    • 精确给出有界轨道的初始值，并给出无界轨道的初等判据。
    💡 分类三个二次 Collatz 型映射的轨道，确定有界轨道的初始值。
    📖 中文摘要

    我们研究非负整数上的三个二次 Collatz 型映射。每个映射将偶数减半，并将奇数 n 分别映到 n(n-1)/2、n(n+1)/2 和 (n^2-1)/4。沿轨道，奇数项增长直到达到形如 2^m+1 或 2^m-1 的数（取决于映射），且具有大于 1 的奇数项的轨道恰好在此发生时是有界的。达到此类数的步骤给出指数 Diophantine 方程的解，配方后变为方程 2^a ± 2^b + 1 = z^2。利用 Szalay 对该方程解的分类，我们分类了映射的所有轨道。每个轨道要么最终周期要么趋于无穷，我们精确确定有界轨道的初始值。对于奇数分支为 n(n-1)/2 的映射，Sedaghat 陈述了分类，我们给出新的完整证明。对于该映射，我们还给出基于整除性的无界轨道的初等判据。据我们所知，其他两个映射的分类是新的。

    🌐 English Abstract

    We study three quadratic Collatz-type maps on the non-negative integers. Each of them halves an even number, and they send an odd $n$ to $n(n-1)/2$, to $n(n+1)/2$ and to $(n^2 - 1)/4$, respectively. Along an orbit, the odd terms grow until they reach a number of the form $2^m + 1$ or $2^m - 1$, depending on the map, and an orbit with an odd term greater than $1$ is bounded exactly when this happens. The step that reaches such a number gives a solution of an exponential Diophantine equation, which becomes the equation $2^a \pm 2^b + 1 = z^2$ after completing the square. With Szalay's classification of the solutions of this equation, we classify all orbits of the maps. Every orbit either is eventually periodic or tends to infinity, and we determine exactly the initial values with bounded orbits. For the map with odd branch $n(n-1)/2$, Sedaghat stated the classification, and we give a new and complete proof. For this map, we also give an elementary criterion for unbounded orbits, based on divisibility alone. To our knowledge, the classifications for the other two maps are new.

    👥 Junxian Li, Xiannan Li, Yongxiao Lin

More precisely, we obtain an asympotic formula with a {\em power-saving} error term for the product of these central values averaged over $\{\chi\bmod q: q\in \mathcal Q\}$ where $\mathcal Q$ is a set of squarefree integers with a small divisor of suitable size.

    📖 中文摘要

    设 f∈Q[t] 是正次数不可约多项式，具有正首项系数，且在每个整数处取整数值。我们证明存在常数 c_f>0，使得对每个充分大的实数 X，区间 (X/2,X] 包含至少 c_f log X log log X 个连续整数 n，使得所有值 f(n) 都是合数。log log X 的指数与 f 的次数无关。这改进了 Ford 和 Gabdullin 先前的结果，将其 log log X 的小固定幂替换为完整因子 log log X。特别地，结果适用于 f(t)=t^2+1。

We prove that there is a constant $c_f>0$ such that, for every sufficiently large real $X$, the interval $(X/2,X]$ contains at least $c_f\log X\log\log X$ consecutive integers $n$ for which all values $f(n)$ are composite. The exponent of $\log\log X$ is independent of the degree of $f$. This improves the previous result of Ford and Gabdullin by replacing their small fixed power of $\log\log X$ with the full factor $\log\log X$. In particular, the result applies to $f(t)=t^2+1$.

    Cardinalities of Jordan Diophantine sets of upper triangular integer matrices
    👥 Zrinka Franušić, Tomislav Pejković
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 研究上三角整数矩阵中 Jordan D(N)-集的基数。
    • 确定存在无限集的 N 的条件。
    • 证明有限集至多六个元素，特殊情形至多五个，且界最优。
    • 给出无限集存在的充要条件。
    💡 确定上三角整数矩阵中无限 Jordan D(N)-集存在的条件，并给出有限集的最大基数。
    📖 中文摘要

    设 U 为上三角 2×2 整数矩阵环。对 N∈U，Jordan D(N)-集是 U 中不同非零矩阵的集合，使得对任意两个不同元素 A 和 B，(AB+BA)/2+N 是 U 中的平方。我们确定所有存在无限 Jordan D(N)-集的 N。若不存在无限集，则每个这样的集至多六个元素。若 N 不是 U 中两个平方的差，则界为五。两个界都是最优的。我们还给出存在具有非零两两 Jordan 积的无限集和由非奇异矩阵组成的无限集的充要条件。

    🌐 English Abstract

    Let $U$ be the ring of upper triangular $2\times2$ integer matrices. For $N\in U$, a Jordan $D(N)$-set is a set of distinct nonzero matrices in $U$ such that $(AB+BA)/2+N$ is a square in $U$ for any two distinct elements $A$ and $B$. We determine all $N$ for which an infinite Jordan $D(N)$-set exists. If no infinite set exists, every such set has at most six elements. If $N$ is not a difference of two squares in $U$, the bound is five. Both bounds are best possible. We also give necessary and sufficient conditions for the existence of infinite sets with nonzero pairwise Jordan products and of infinite sets consisting of nonsingular matrices.

    📖 中文摘要

    受丢番图逼近应用的启发，我们为 R^n 中的非退化流形发展了一种新的纤维化机制，表明它们允许局部分解为非退化曲线族，并具有一致定量非退化性质。流形需为 C^N，其中 N ≥ max{n, l+1} 本质上最优，这里流形是 l 非退化的。此外，我们证明这种一致定量局部控制可以全局化为定性陈述，使得非退化流形的几乎每一点都位于流形内的一条非退化曲线上。我们的方法是微扰的，表明解析设置中出现的纤维化结构在足够小的 C^N 微扰下是稳定的。该方法为将丢番图逼近中基于纤维化的结果从曲线推广到流形提供了框架。本文考虑了此类应用。

    🌐 English Abstract

    Motivated by applications in Diophantine approximation, we develop a new fibering mechanism for non-degenerate manifolds in $\mathbb{R}^n$, showing that they admit local decompositions into families of non-degenerate curves with uniform quantitative non-degeneracy properties. The manifolds are required to be $C^N$ with the essentially optimal bound $N\geq \max\lbrace n,l+1\rbrace$, where the manifold is $l$ non-degenerate. Furthermore, we show that this uniform quantitative local control can be globalised into a qualitative statement, so that almost every point of a non-degenerate manifold lies on a non-degenerate curve contained in the manifold. Our approach is perturbative and shows that the fibering structure arising in the analytic setting is stable under sufficiently small $C^N$ perturbations. The method provides a framework for extending fibering based results in Diophantine approximation from curves to manifolds. Such applications are considered in the paper.

    Splitting fields of complex hyperbolic groups: matrix extraction, Hermitian descent, and spectral fields
    👥 Angel Cano, Hector Castro, Nikolay Gusevskii
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 研究复双曲群的分裂域问题。
    • 证明迹和谱给出线性实现。
    • 发现 Hermitian 相似障碍。
    • 在 SU(3,1) 中构造反例。
    • 算术群下障碍消失。
    💡 研究复双曲群的分裂域问题，给出线性实现和 Hermitian 下降障碍。
    📖 中文摘要

    设 Γ < SU(n,1)，n ≥ 2，是 Zariski 稠密的且包含正则斜驶元素 A。分裂域问题问迹和谱在多大程度上决定 Γ 的定义域。我们首先证明迹连同 A 的谱总是给出 K = Q(trΓ, E(A)) 上的线性实现。我们还证明 SU(n,1) 的每个复不可约子群若实迹域为实数，则共轭入 SO(n,1)；特别地，此实迹结论不需要离散性假设。然而在高维，线性下降本身不给出标准酉形式：存在 Hermitian 相似障碍，在虚二次域上由 Landherr 局部不变量描述。在 SU(3,1) 中我们给出一个 Zariski 稠密例子，其单谱域为 Q(i) 但不产生标准酉形式。这自然导致全谱域。我们证明对于算术群和在适当局部开性假设下障碍消失，并给出偶次局部判据。在四维我们通过显式 Schottky 群和秩一局部几何分析离散分支。那里完整 Bruhat 余圈恢复各向异性 Levi 完整并强制非分裂旋转字。剩余准分裂秩二情形未被此方法触及，因此一般 SU(3,1) 问题仍开放。

    🌐 English Abstract

    Let $\Gamma<\mathrm{SU}(n,1)$, $n\ge2$, be Zariski-dense and contain a regular loxodromic element $A$. The splitting-field problem asks how far traces and spectra determine a field of definition for $\Gamma$. We show first that traces together with the spectrum of $A$ always give a linear realization over $K=\mathbb{Q}(\textrm{tr}\Gamma,E(A))$. We also prove that every complex-irreducible subgroup of $\mathrm{SU}(n,1)$ with real trace field is conjugate into $\mathrm{SO}(n,1)$; in particular, no discreteness hypothesis is required for this real-trace conclusion. In higher dimension, however, linear descent does not by itself give the standard unitary form: a Hermitian similarity obstruction remains, described over imaginary quadratic fields by Landherr's local invariants. In $\mathrm{SU}(3,1)$ we give a Zariski-dense example for which the one-spectrum field is $\mathbb{Q}(i)$ but does not yield the standard unitary form. This leads naturally to the total spectral field. We prove that the obstruction disappears for arithmetic groups and under suitable local openness hypotheses, and we give an even-degree local criterion. In dimension four we analyze the discrete branch through explicit Schottky groups and rank-one local geometry. There the full Bruhat cocycle recovers anisotropic Levi holonomy and forces a non-split rotational word. The remaining quasi-split rank-two case is not reached by this method, so the general $\mathrm{SU}(3,1)$ problem remains open.

    👉 https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10894

    • 联系大值估计改进。
    💡 研究短区间中算术函数与线性相位的相关性，突破 3/5 障碍。
    📖 中文摘要

    我们引入了一种新方法来研究短区间中算术函数与高度无理频率的线性相位之间的相关性。作为应用，我们证明当 x^{0.58+ε} ≤ H ≤ x 时，|∑_{x<n≤x+H} μ(n) e(nα)| ≪ H (log x)^{-1/3+ε}。在相同的 H 范围内，我们还证明 |∑_{x<n≤x+H} Λ(n) e(nα)| ≪ H (log x)^{-A} 对任意大的 A>0 成立，除非存在整数 1 ≤ q ≪ (log x)^{O_A(1)} 使得 ||qα|| ≪ x (log x)^{O_A(1)}/H^2。这打破了 arXiv:1911.09076v2 定理 1.5 和 Zhan 定理 2 中的 3/5 障碍。此外，通过我们的方法，对特征扭曲 Dirichlet 多项式大值估计的任何改进都将导致 H 下界的相应改进。

    🌐 English Abstract

    We introduce a new approach to studying correlations between arithmetic functions and linear phases with respect to highly irrational frequency over short intervals. As an application, we prove that \[ \Big|\sum_{x<n \leq x+H}\mu(n) e(n\alpha)\Big| \ll H(\log x)^{-1/3+\varepsilon } \] whenever $x^{0.58+\varepsilon }\leq H\leq x$. In the same range of $H$ we also show that \[ \Big|\sum_{x<n \leq x+H} \Lambda (n) e(n\alpha)\Big| \ll H(\log x)^{-A} \] for arbitrarily large $A>0$ unless there exists an integer $1\leq q\ll (\log x)^{O_A(1)}$ such that $\|q\alpha \| \ll x (\log x)^{O_A(1)}/H^2$. This breaks the $3/5$ barrier in Theorem 1.5 of arXiv:1911.09076v2 and Theorem 2 of [T. Zhan, "On the representation of large odd integer as a sum of three almost equal primes," Acta Mathematica Sinica 7.3 (1991), 259-272]. Moreover, by our method, any improvement in large value estimates for character-twisted Dirichlet polynomials would lead to a corresponding improvement in the lower bound for $H$.

  27. [2610.11648] p 进同余与序列 p 进插值的统一框架
    A Unified Framework for $p$-adic Congruences and $p$-adic Interpolations of Sequences
    👥 Yuta Nishibuchi
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 研究序列的 p 进同余和插值。
    • 建立统一代数框架。
    • 避免使用 p 进积分。
    • 应用于 Genocchi 数等。
    💡 建立统一框架研究序列的 p 进同余和插值函数的局部解析性。
    📖 中文摘要

    在本文中，我们处理确定具有指数生成函数 F(t) 的序列 {c_n} 何时满足同余 c_{n+(p^h-1)p^k} ≡ c_n mod p^{k+r}（对大 n）以及其标准插值函数何时局部解析的问题。这种同余常见于若干数论或组合序列，如 Bernoulli 数、Euler 数和 Fibonacci 数。传统上，此类同余通过 p 进积分证明。本文提出另一种方法。我们不用 p 进积分，而是研究生成函数集合的代数结构，并建立 F(t) 的某些条件，这些条件等价于或充分于同余和插值函数的光滑性。我们还提供了框架对 Genocchi 数、广义 Euler 数等的具体应用，这些超出了典型 p 进积分方法的范围。

    🌐 English Abstract

    In this paper, we treat the problem of determining when a sequence $\{c_n\}_{n=0}^{\infty}$ with the exponential generating function $F(t)$ satisfies the congruence $c_{n+(p^h-1)p^k}\equiv c_n \pmod{p^{k+r}}$ for large $n$ and when its canonical interpolating functions are locally analytic. This type of congruence is often observed for several number-theoretic or combinatorial sequences, such as Bernoulli numbers, Euler numbers, and Fibonacci numbers. Traditionally, such congruences are proved by using $p$-adic integrations. In this paper, we present another approach to this problem. Instead of using $p$-adic integrals, we investigate an algebraic structure of the set of generating functions and establish some conditions on $F(t)$ that are equivalent to or sufficient for the congruence and smoothness of the interpolating functions. We also provide concrete applications of our framework to Genocchi numbers, generalized Euler numbers, and so on, which are outside the scope of the typical $p$-adic integration method.

    👉 https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11648

    👥 Tianyu Ni
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 研究 L 函数素数二次扭曲的非消没。
    • 证明比例趋于零且一致。
    • 使用随机 Euler 乘积非集中定理。
    • 应用于生成和密度一基结果。
    💡 证明标准与对称平方 L 函数素数二次扭曲的一致非消没，并应用于基。
    📖 中文摘要

    设 S_k 为 SL_2(Z) 权 k 尖形式空间，dim S_k ≥ 1，χ_p = (·/p) 对奇素数 p。对固定 s 且 Re(s) > (k+1)/2，我们证明满足 L(h, χ_p, s) = 0 的素数 p ≤ X 的比例当 X → ∞ 时趋于零，且对非零 h ∈ S_k 一致。我们还证明了归一化 Hecke 特征形式的扭曲对称平方 L 值的非平凡线性组合的类似陈述，当 Re(s) > k。证明的主要成分是随机 Euler 乘积在成对局部分离条件下的非集中定理。作为应用，我们获得 S_k 中与扭曲标准和对称平方 L 函数相关的核的生成和密度一基结果，当 dim S_k = 2 或 3 时在指定右半平面有显式基判据。我们证明几乎每 dim S_k 个素数的元组从每个非中心临界指数的扭曲周期给出 S_k^* 的基。我们还证明了从素数水平 Eisenstein 级数的 Rankin-Cohen 括号的迹获得的 S_k 有理基的密度一结果。

    🌐 English Abstract

    Let $S_k$ be the space of cusp forms of weight $k$ for ${\rm SL}_2(\mathbb Z)$, with $\dim S_k\geq1$, and let $\chi_p=\left(\frac{\cdot}{p}\right)$ for odd primes $p$. For fixed $s$ with ${\rm Re}(s)>(k+1)/2$, we prove that the proportion of primes $p\leq X$ satisfying $L(h,\chi_p,s)=0$ tends to zero as $X\to\infty$, uniformly over nonzero $h\in S_k$. We also prove an analogous statement for nontrivial linear combinations of twisted symmetric square $L$-values of the normalized Hecke eigenforms when ${\rm Re}(s)>k$. The main ingredient of the proof is a non-concentration theorem for random Euler products under a pairwise local separation condition. As applications, we obtain spanning and density-one basis results in $S_k$ for the kernels associated with twisted standard and symmetric square $L$-functions, with explicit basis criteria in specified right half-planes when $\dim S_k=2$ or $3$. We show that almost every tuple of $\dim S_k$ primes gives a basis of $S_k^{\ast}$ from twisted periods at every noncentral critical index. We also prove a density-one result for rational bases of $S_k$ obtained from traces of Rankin-Cohen brackets of Eisenstein series at prime levels.

  33. [2610.12013] 通过 Iwasawa 轨迹的 CM 曲线单能 Selmer 维数
    Unipotent Selmer dimensions for CM curves via Iwasawa loci
    👥 Ander Martin
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 研究 CM 曲线的单能 Selmer 维数。
    • 证明 Kim 猜想特例。
    • 使用 Iwasawa 轨迹和 p 进解析函数。
    • 通过测度收敛和紧性论证。
    💡 通过 Iwasawa 轨迹研究 CM 曲线的单能 Selmer 维数，证明 Kim 猜想特例。
    📖 中文摘要

    设 X/Q 是光滑、射影、几何整的亏格 g ≥ 2 曲线，有点 b ∈ X(Q) 且几何 CM Jacobian。固定好约化的奇素数 p，设 U 为 X 的 étale 前单能 Q_p 基本群。我们证明对所有 n ≫ 0，Bloch-Kato Selmer 方案的不等式 dim_{Q_p} H^1_f(G_T, U_n) < dim_{Q_p} H^1_f(G_p, U_n)，其比值的上极限至多 1/2。这里 U_n 是下中心列商，T 是包含 p 和坏约化素数的有限集。这回答了 Kim 猜想的一个特例。遵循 Coates-Kim 的 Iwasawa 理论方法，我们将问题归约到界某些特征在 n 次的多重性。然后定义扭曲特征格 B 及其 p 进完备 B_p。主要思想是将归一化多重性视为 μ_n({x ∈ B_p: f(x,n)=0})，其中 f 来自 Iwasawa 理论的 p 进解析函数，测度序列 (μ_n)_n 弱收敛到 B_p 上的 Haar 概率测度 μ，即均匀测度的 p 进类似。我们通过考虑 B_p 的特征 η 并比较 ∫_{B_p} η dμ_n 与由辛算子 h_η 诱导的算子的迹，然后使用特征公式来证明收敛。p 进轨迹序列的紧性论证给出主要 Iwasawa 估计。Poitou-Tate 对偶、全局 Euler 特征公式和 Hodge 滤过估计然后给出维数不等式。

    🌐 English Abstract

    Let $X/\mathbb{Q}$ be a smooth, projective, geometrically integral curve of genus $g \geq 2$ with a point $ b \in X(\mathbb{Q})$ and geometrically CM Jacobian. Fix an odd prime $p$ of good reduction, and let $U$ be the \'etale pro-unipotent $\mathbb{Q}_p$-fundamental group of $X$. We prove, for all $n \gg 0,$ the inequality $\dim_{\mathbb{Q}_p} H^1_f(G_T, U_n) < \dim_{\mathbb{Q}_p}H^1_f(G_p, U_n)$ of Bloch--Kato Selmer schemes, with their ratio having limit superior at most $1/2$. Here $U_n$ is the lower central series quotient, and $T$ a finite set containing $p$ and the primes of bad reduction. This answers a particular case of a conjecture by Kim. Following the Iwasawa-theoretic method of Coates--Kim, we reduce the problem to bounding certain character multiplicities at degree $n$. We then define a twisted character lattice $B$ and its $p$-adic completion $B_p.$ Our main idea is to regard normalized multiplicities as $\mu_n(\lbrace x \in B_p: f(x,n)=0\rbrace)$ for a $p$-adic analytic function $f$ coming from Iwasawa theory and a sequence of measures $(\mu_n)_n$ weakly converging to the Haar probability measure $\mu$ on $B_p,$ which is the $p$-adic analogue of the uniform measure. We prove convergence by considering characters $\eta$ of $B_p$ and comparing $\int_{B_p} \eta \ d\mu_n$ to traces of operators induced by symplectic operators $h_\eta$, followed by a character formula. A compactness argument for sequences of $p$-adic loci gives our main Iwasawa estimate. Poitou-Tate duality, the global Euler characteristic formula, and Hodge filtration estimates then give the dimension inequality.

The multiplier bound is uniform in the pair and asymptotically optimal up to a small multiplicative constant. Given quaternionic coordinates, we provide a deterministic algorithm that satisfies this upper bound in polynomial time. For unrestricted multipliers, $\mathrm{KLPT}^{2}$ provides a heuristic algorithm with a multiplier upper bound of order $p^{6+o(1)}$ for arbitrary pairs and $p^{3+o(1)}$ when one polarization matrix is the identity matrix. Our algorithm's improvement is an explicit upper bound below $p$ with an unconditional deterministic guarantee.

    🌐 English Abstract

  44. [2610.11687] 单位环面上随机点数目方差的渐近行为
    On the asymptotic behavior of the number variance of random points on the unit torus
    👥 Christoph Aistleitner, Siegfried Hörmann, Maryna Manskova
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 研究单位环面上独立随机点数目方差的分布与渐近行为。
    • 高精度计算广泛参数范围内数目方差的分布。
    • 确定 Poisson 渐近 V_N(s)~Ns 失效的精确阈值。
    • 关键技术来自 Komlós–Major–Tusnády 定理和 Karhunen-Loève 分解。
    💡 精确计算单位环面上独立随机点数目方差的分布，确定 Poisson 渐近 V_N(s)~Ns 失效的阈值。
    📖 中文摘要

    单位环面上实序列分布的研究历史悠久。许多概念描述给定确定性序列（常来自算术）的「伪随机」行为，如等分布（全局尺度）或对相关（局部尺度）。另一个重要统计量是数目方差 V_N(s)，描述序列初始段在长度 s 的区间内元素个数在环面上移动时的均方涨落，适用于从全局到局部的所有尺度。在量子混沌中，伪随机行为称为 Poisson 行为。由于数目方差是判定伪随机行为的重要指标，文献中竟缺乏真正随机情形（独立同分布随机点）下数目方差分布和几乎必然渐近行为的精确描述，即使在单位环面均匀分布这一相对简单的设定中也是如此。本文中，我们在广泛的参数 s 范围内高精度计算数目方差的分布，并确定典型随机序列中「Poisson」渐近 V_N(s)~Ns 失效的精确阈值。关键技术输入来自 Komlós–Major–Tusnády 定理和 Karhunen-Loève 分解。

    🌐 English Abstract

    The study of the distribution of real sequences in the unit torus has a long and rich history. There are many notions which describe the ``pseudo-random'' behavior of a given deterministic sequence (often of arithmetic origin), such as equidistribution (which describes the distribution on a ``global scale'') or the pair correlation (which is a ``local-scale'' statistics). Another popular and important statistics, which can be applied to all ranges from global via intermediate to local, is the number variance $V_N(s)$, which describes the mean-square fluctuation of the number of elements of an initial segment of the sequence in an interval of length $s$, when moving this interval around the torus. In the terminology of quantum chaology, where such statistics are used to describe the distribution of the energy levels of a system, pseudo-random behavior is called Poissonian behavior. Since the number variance is an important indicator to qualify pseudo-random behavior, it is quite surprising that the literature does not seem to contain a precise description of the distribution and of the almost sure asymptotic behavior of the number variance in the truly random case (i.i.d.\ random points), even in the comparatively simple setup of uniformly distributed points on the unit torus. In the present paper we calculate the distribution of the number variance with high precision throughout a wide range of the parameter $s$, and determine the precise threshold (in terms of $s$) where the ``Poissonian'' asymptotics $V_N(s) \sim N s$ breaks for a typical realization of a random sequence. Key technical input comes from the Koml\'{o}s--Major--Tusn\'{a}dy theorem and the Karhunen-Lo\`eve decomposition.

    👉 https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11687

    We introduce an asymmetric variant of a classic problem by Roth about colourings of integers and midpoints. The new problem cannot be solved with the standard approach by Erd\"os-S\'ark\"ozy-S\'os which uses symmetry. We describe a Fourier Analytic approach which gives asymptotically tight bounds. In the $\mathbb{F}_3^n$ setting, the density increment we show is efficient and together with the Freiman-Ruzsa Theorem provides an affine subspace of near optimal codimension within the distinguished set. This approach adopted to the setting of the integers from $1$ to $N$ via Bohr sets and Bogolyubov-Ruzsa Lemma gives a progression of length being a power of $N$ which only depends on the number of colours. We then use Chang's Lemma and Balog-Szemer\'edi-Gowers Theorem to further improve the dependence on the number of colours.

Moreover, given a finite \'etale map of regular schemes $f \colon Y \to X$ that is curve tame over a base $S$, we construct a compactification $\bar{f} \colon \overline{Y} \to \overline{X}$ over $S$ that is numerically tame.

    A Complete Proof of Mueller--Ho Conjecture
    👥 Senping Luo, Juncheng Wei
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 完整证明 Bose–Einstein 凝聚中的 Mueller–Ho 猜想。
    • 研究经典与移位 theta 函数加权和的最小值问题。
    • 确定三个阈值，给出最小值随参数 α 变化的完整分段表达式。
    • 复变量和向量分别表示双组分 Bose 气体的涡旋形状和相对位置。
    💡 完整证明 Bose–Einstein 凝聚中 Mueller–Ho 猜想，确定经典与移位 theta 函数加权和的最小值随参数 α 变化的三个阈值。
    📖 中文摘要

    对 s>0 和 z∈H={z=x+iy: y>0}，定义 θ(s;z)=∑_{m,n∈Z} e^{-πs(m²y+(mx-n)²/y)} 和 J(z;a,b)=∑_{m,n∈Z} e^{-π(m²y+(mx-n)²/y)} cos(2π(ma+nb))，分别为经典和移位 theta 函数（高斯格和）。我们证明，在模群意义下，存在三个阈值 0<α_a<α_b<α_c<1，使得 Minima_{z∈H,(a,b)∈R²}(θ(1;z)+αJ(z;a,b)) 等于：当 α∈[0,α_a) 时为 (e^{iπ/3};1/3,1/3)；当 α=α_a 时为 (e^{iπ/3};1/3,1/3) 或 (e^{iφ_{α_a}};1/2,1/2)；当 α∈(α_a,α_b) 时为 (e^{iφ_α};1/2,1/2)，φ_α∈(φ_{α_a},π/2)；当 α∈[α_b,α_c] 时为 (i;1/2,1/2)；当 α∈(α_c,1] 时为 (iy_α;1/2,1/2)，y_α∈(1,√3]，y_1=√3。其中复变量 z 和向量 (a,b) 分别表示双组分 Bose 气体的总涡旋形状和相对位置。由此完全证明了 Mueller 和 Ho 于 2002 年提出的源于 Bose–Einstein 凝聚的猜想。

    🌐 English Abstract

    For $s>0$, and $z\in\mathbb H=\{z=x+iy:y>0\}$, define $\theta(s;z)=\sum_{m,n\in\mathbb Z}e^{-\pi s\big(m^2y+\frac{(mx-n)^2}{y}\big)}$ and $ J(z;a,b)=\sum_{m,n\in\mathbb Z}e^{-\pi \big(m^2y+\frac{(mx-n)^2}{y}\big)} \cos\bigl(2\pi(ma+nb)\bigr)$ for the classical and shifted theta functions (Gaussian lattice sums), respectively. We prove that, up to the modular group, there exist three thresholds $0<\alpha_a<\alpha_b<\alpha_c<1$ such that \begin{equation}\aligned\nonumber \Minima_{z\in\mathbb H,\,(a,b)\in\mathbb R^2}\Big(\theta(1;z)+\alpha J(z;a,b)\Big)= \begin{cases} \;\big(e^{i{\pi}/{3}};1/3,1/3\big), &\hbox{if}\;\; \alpha\in[0,\alpha_a),\\ \;\big(e^{i{\pi}/{3}};1/3,1/3\big)\;\hbox{or}\;\big(e^{i{\varphi_{\alpha_a}}};1/2,1/2\big), &\hbox{if}\;\; \alpha=\alpha_a,\\ \;\big(e^{i{\varphi_\alpha}};1/2,1/2\big),\;\varphi_\alpha\in(\varphi_{\alpha_a},\frac{\pi}{2}),\;\; &\hbox{if}\;\; \alpha\in(\alpha_a,\alpha_b),\\ \;\big(i;1/2,1/2\big),\; &\hbox{if}\;\; \alpha\in[\alpha_b,\alpha_c],\\ \;\big(iy_\alpha;1/2,1/2\big),\; y_\alpha\in(1,\sqrt3],\;\;y_1=\sqrt3,\;\;&\hbox{if}\;\; \alpha\in(\alpha_c,1], \end{cases} \endaligned\end{equation} where the complex variable $z$ and vector $(a,b)$, represent the total vortex shapes and relative positions of the two-component Bose gas, respectively. Consequently, this completely proves the conjecture of Mueller and Ho~\cite{Mue2002} (2002) arising in Bose--Einstein condensates.

    Twisted Jacquet modules and induction from Speh representations
    👥 Alan Xuelun Hou
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 研究从 Speh 表示诱导的扭曲 Jacquet 模的局部结构。
    • 确定 (J_s⊗σ)_G 在有限例外参数外由 Δ(τ,i)|det|^s 和 σ 的共轭诱导。
    • 对不可约 σ 确定所有不可约商，并给出例外参数显式有限集。
    • 证明辛双覆盖类似结果，并在大特征有限域上给出完全分解。
    💡 确定非阿基米德局部域上从 Speh 表示诱导的扭曲 Jacquet 模的局部模结构，证明其与 σ 的诱导关系并给出例外参数集。
    📖 中文摘要

    从 Speh 表示诱导的 Eisenstein 级数的 Fourier 系数是 Cai、Friedberg、Ginzburg 和 Kaplan 广义加倍方法的核心。Ginzburg 和 Soudry 将这些系数与尖点形式积分，得到从较短 Speh 表示和任意尖点数据诱导的 Eisenstein 级数。我们确定特征零非阿基米德局部域 F 上对应的局部模。设 q_F 为其剩余域基数。设 J_s 为从 Δ(τ,m+i)|det|^s 诱导表示的扭曲 Jacquet 模。它带有 G×H 的作用，其中 G 和 H 是分裂辛群或特殊正交群。设 σ 为 G 的有限长容许表示。我们证明 (J_s⊗σ)_G 在 q_F^{-s} 的有限例外值集之外，是从 Δ(τ,i)|det|^s 和 σ 的固定共轭诱导的。不需要 σ 的一般性假设。对不可约 σ，这确定了 J_s 的所有形如 σ^∨⊠π 的不可约商。证明计算了每个轨道贡献，包括因尖点性整体消失的贡献，并给出包含例外参数的显式有限集。对不可约超尖点 σ，除非 G 是分裂群 SO_2，该集为空。我们证明辛双覆盖的类似结果。在大特征有限域上，当 τ 是 GL_n 上的尖点表示且 n 大于 G 的秩时，我们给出扭曲 Jacquet 模的完全分解。

    🌐 English Abstract

    Fourier coefficients of Eisenstein series induced from Speh representations are the kernels of the generalized doubling method of Cai, Friedberg, Ginzburg and Kaplan. Ginzburg and Soudry integrated these coefficients against cusp forms to obtain Eisenstein series induced from shorter Speh representations and arbitrary cuspidal data. We determine the corresponding local modules over a non-archimedean local field $F$ of characteristic zero. Let $q_F$ be the cardinality of its residue field. Let $J_s$ be the twisted Jacquet module of the representation induced from $\Delta(\tau,m+i)|\det|^s$. It carries an action of $G\times H$, where $G$ and $H$ are split symplectic or special orthogonal groups. Let $\sigma$ be an admissible representation of $G$ of finite length. We prove that $(J_s\otimes\sigma)_G$ is induced from $\Delta(\tau,i)|\det|^s$ and a fixed conjugate of $\sigma$, outside a finite set of values of $q_F^{-s}$ depending on $\tau$ and $\sigma$. No genericity assumption on $\sigma$ is needed. For irreducible $\sigma$, this determines all irreducible quotients of $J_s$ of the form $\sigma^\vee\boxtimes\pi$. The proof computes every orbit contribution, including those that vanish globally by cuspidality. It also gives an explicit finite set containing the exceptional parameters. This set is empty for irreducible supercuspidal $\sigma$ unless $G$ is the split group $\mathrm{SO}_2$. We prove the analogous result for the symplectic double cover. Over finite fields of large characteristic, we give the complete decomposition of the twisted Jacquet module when $\tau$ is cuspidal on $\mathrm{GL}_n$ and $n$ is larger than the rank of $G$.

    💡 研究代数群在 Poineau 混合 Berkovich 紧化上的作用，刻画作用良定义的集合，证明商同胚于紧化，并应用于有理映射空间。
    📖 中文摘要

    设 X 为 C 上代数簇，G 为作用在 X 上且作用闭的代数群。J. Poineau 利用混合 Berkovich 空间定义了 X(C) 的紧化 X^⌝。我们关注 G 的作用在该紧化上的延拓，刻画作用良定义的集合 U⊂X^⌝。我们还证明 U 对 G 作用的商同胚于 (X/G)^⌝，即 (X/G)(C) 的紧化。随后我们将这些结果应用于 X=Rat_d（有理映射空间）和 G=SL_2，在更一般的设定下给出 C. Favre-C. Gong 的结果。此外，我们得到 M_d=Rat_d/SL_2 的紧化，其边界由非阿基米德有理映射的轨道组成。若将 C 替换为非平凡赋值域 k，复解析空间替换为 k 上 Berkovich 空间，或 X 为 GIT 意义下 k-簇的稳定点集，结果仍然成立。

    🌐 English Abstract

    Let $X$ be an algebraic variety over $\mathbb{C}$ and $G$ be an algebraic group acting on $X$ whose action is closed. J. Poineau defined a compactification $X^\urcorner$ of $X(\mathbb{C})$ by using hybrid Berkovich spaces. We will focus on the extension of the action of $G$ on this compactification by characterising the set $\mathcal{U} \subset X^\urcorner$ where the action is well defined. We will also show that the quotient of $\mathcal{U}$ by the action of $G$ is homeomorphic to $(X/G)^\urcorner$, the compactification of $(X/G)(\mathbb{C})$. We then apply these results to $X = \mathrm{Rat}_d$, the space of rational maps and $G = \mathrm{SL}_2$. It gives the results of C. Favre-C. Gong in a more general setting. Furthermore, we get a compactification of $\mathrm{M}_d = \mathrm{Rat}_d/\mathrm{SL}_2$ where the boundary is made of orbits of non-archimedean rational maps. The results still holds if $\mathbb{C}$ is replaced by $k$ a non-trivially valued field and complex analytic spaces by Berkovich spaces over $k$ or if $X$ is the set of stable points of a $k$-variety defined in the sense of GIT.

Using tools from homogeneous dynamics, we study this problem by reducing it to a shrinking target problem on certain homogeneous spaces of $\mathrm{SL}_2(\mathbb{C})$. The main novel ingredients include measure estimates on a certain family of neighborhoods of the corresponding critical loci, as well as new disjointness statements to control the short-range mixing contribution. Due to the different nature of the critical loci in the Gaussian and Eisenstein cases, these measure estimates are obtained by rather different arguments.
